```lua
        for i = 2, #pts do
          if dist_seg(x, z, pts[i - 1][1], pts[i - 1][2], pts[i][1], pts[i][2]) < (b.width or 3) / 2 + 0.5 then return false end
        end
      end
      local e, _, _, _, d = R.nearest_edge(x, z)
      return not (e and d < NET.edges[e].width / 2 + 1)
    end
  end
  return false
end

-- Is the pavement on side `side` of edge e (+1: right of its first bend
-- toward its last, classify's side 1) clear to walk? Not where it lies on
-- another road's asphalt, as between the two halves of a divided road with
-- little or no median: no more than a fifth of the way.
function R.pavement(e, side)
  NET.pave = NET.pave or {}
  local key = e * 2 + (side > 0 and 1 or 0)
  if NET.pave[key] == nil then
    local pieces, s, bad = NET.edges[e].pieces or {}, side > 0 and 1 or 2, 0
    for _, pc in ipairs(pieces) do
      local sd = pc.side[s]
      if sd.overlap or (sd.kind == "median" and sd.w < 0.9) then bad = bad + 1 end
    end
    NET.pave[key] = bad <= #pieces * 0.2
  end
  return NET.pave[key]
end

-- Pavement for pedestrians: the edges they can walk (not highways, not roads
-- explicitly tagged with no sidewalks, and with a clear pavement on at least one side).
function R.walkable(e)
  local ed = NET.edges[e]
  if ed.kind == "highway" or ed.sidewalk == "no" then return false end
  return (R.pavement(e, 1) or R.pavement(e, -1))
end
R.SIDEWALK = SIDEWALK

return R

```

--- FILE: game/lib/hud.lua ---
```lua
-- Heads-up display: cash, speed, mission text, minimap, big messages.
local G = require "lib.state"
local U = require "lib.util"
local settings = require "lib.settings"

local M = {}

local function shadow_text(s, x, y, size, r, g, b)
  -- 4-way dark outline drop shadow for maximum legibility against bright sky, water, and glare
  engine.text(s, x - 1, y, size, 0, 0, 0, 0.75)
  engine.text(s, x + 1, y, size, 0, 0, 0, 0.75)
  engine.text(s, x, y - 1, size, 0, 0, 0, 0.75)
  engine.text(s, x, y + 1, size, 0, 0, 0, 0.75)
  engine.text(s, x + 1.5, y + 1.5, size, 0, 0, 0, 0.85)
  engine.text(s, x, y, size, r, g, b, 1)
end

local function centered(s, y, size, r, g, b)
  local w = engine.screen()
  shadow_text(s, (w - engine.text_width(s, size)) / 2, y, size, r, g, b)
end

local function bar(x, y, bw, bh, frac, r, g, b)
  engine.rect(x - 2, y - 2, bw + 4, bh + 4, 0, 0, 0, 0.6)
  engine.rect(x, y, bw * math.max(0, math.min(1, frac)), bh, r, g, b, 0.95)
end

-- The map's static part (water, blocks, park, roads) as rects in 0..1 map
-- coordinates, worked out once per city.
local base, base_city
local COLORS = {
  downtown = {0.62, 0.62, 0.6}, residential = {0.7, 0.62, 0.52}, industrial = {0.62, 0.5, 0.38},
  park = {0.36, 0.58, 0.3}, road = {0.25, 0.25, 0.27}, land = {0.3, 0.42, 0.26}, water = {0.18, 0.32, 0.55},
}
local function map_base(L)
  if base_city == L then return base end
  local city = require "lib.city"
  local rects = {}
  local function add(x0, z0, x1, z1, c)
    rects[#rects + 1] = {(x0 - (L.cx or 0) + L.sx / 2) / L.sx, (z0 - (L.cz or 0) + L.sz / 2) / L.sz, (x1 - x0) / L.sx, (z1 - z0) / L.sz, c}
  end
  if L.network then
    -- Land, then rivers and roads as chains of dots, so curves show.
    add((L.cx or 0) - L.sx / 2, (L.cz or 0) - L.sz / 2, (L.cx or 0) + L.sx / 2, (L.cz or 0) + L.sz / 2, COLORS.land)
    local function dots(pts, w, c)
      for i = 2, #pts do
        local ax, az, bx, bz = pts[i - 1][1], pts[i - 1][2], pts[i][1], pts[i][2]
        local n = math.max(1, math.floor(U.dist2d(ax, az, bx, bz) / 6))
        for k = 0, n do
          local x, z = ax + (bx - ax) * k / n, az + (bz - az) * k / n
          add(x - w / 2, z - w / 2, x + w / 2, z + w / 2, c)
        end
      end
    end
    for _, river in ipairs(city.map().rivers or {}) do dots(river, (river.width or 10) * 1.6, COLORS.water) end
    for _, e in ipairs(require("lib.roadnet").net().edges) do dots(e.pts, math.max(9, e.width * 1.3), COLORS.road) end
    base, base_city = rects, L
    return rects
  end
  local A, S = L.avenue / 2, L.street / 2
  for _, j in ipairs(city.junctions()) do
    local x, z = city.pos(j)
    add(x - A, z - S, x + A, z + S, COLORS.road)
  end
  for _, r in ipairs(city.roads()) do
    local ax, az = city.pos(r[1])
    local bx, bz = city.pos(r[2])
    add(math.min(ax, bx) - A, math.min(az, bz) - S, math.max(ax, bx) + A, math.max(az, bz) + S, COLORS.road)
  end
  for i = 0, L.NX - 1 do
    for j = 0, L.NZ - 1 do
      local c = COLORS[city.district(i, j)]
      if c and city.district(i, j) ~= "park" then
        local x, z = city.block_center(i, j)
        add(x - L.bx / 2, z - L.bz / 2, x + L.bx / 2, z + L.bz / 2, c)
      end
    end
  end
  local P = L.park_area
  if P then
    add(P.x0, P.z0, P.x1, P.z1, COLORS.park)
    add(P.x0, P.transverse - S, P.x1, P.transverse + S, COLORS.road)
  end
  base, base_city = rects, L
  return rects
end

-- A town too big to show whole gets a radar instead: RADAR meters each way
-- round the player. Its static part is world-space rects, bucketed in
-- CELL-meter squares so a frame only looks at the ones in view.
local RADAR, CELL = 400, 100
local radar_base, radar_city
local stamp = 0
local function radar_rects(L)
  if radar_city == L then return radar_base end
  local cells = {}
  local function add(x0, z0, x1, z1, c)
    local r = {x0, z0, x1, z1, c}
    for i = math.floor(x0 / CELL), math.floor(x1 / CELL) do
      for j = math.floor(z0 / CELL), math.floor(z1 / CELL) do
        local k = i * 100003 + j
        cells[k] = cells[k] or {}
        table.insert(cells[k], r)
      end
    end
  end
  local city = require "lib.city"
  for _, st in ipairs((city.map().cover or {}).water or {}) do add(st[1], st[2], st[3], st[4], COLORS.water) end
  for _, e in ipairs(require("lib.roadnet").net().edges) do
    local w = math.max(8, e.width * 1.2)
    for i = 2, #e.pts do
      local ax, az, bx, bz = e.pts[i - 1][1], e.pts[i - 1][2], e.pts[i][1], e.pts[i][2]
      local n = math.max(1, math.floor(U.dist2d(ax, az, bx, bz) / (w * 0.6)))
      for k = 0, n do
        local x, z = ax + (bx - ax) * k / n, az + (bz - az) * k / n
        add(x - w / 2, z - w / 2, x + w / 2, z + w / 2, COLORS.road)
      end
    end
  end
  radar_base, radar_city = cells, L
  return cells
end

local function radar(sw, sh, L)
  local px, _, pz = U.player_pos()
  if not px then return end
  local S = math.floor(math.min(230, sh * 0.3))
  local x0, y0 = sw - S - 20, sh - S - 20
  local k = S / (2 * RADAR)
  -- Anything off the radar is pinned to its edge, so blips still point the way.
  local function map(wx, wz)
    local u = math.max(-RADAR, math.min(RADAR, wx - px))
    local v = math.max(-RADAR, math.min(RADAR, wz - pz))
    return x0 + (u + RADAR) * k, y0 + (v + RADAR) * k
  end
  engine.rect(x0 - 4, y0 - 4, S + 8, S + 8, 0, 0, 0, 0.55)
  engine.rect(x0, y0, S, S, COLORS.land[1], COLORS.land[2], COLORS.land[3], 0.9)
  -- Rects span several cells; each is drawn once a frame, marked with the
  -- frame's stamp (no table a frame for the garbage collector).
  local cells = radar_rects(L)
  stamp = stamp + 1
  for i = math.floor((px - RADAR) / CELL), math.floor((px + RADAR) / CELL) do
    for j = math.floor((pz - RADAR) / CELL), math.floor((pz + RADAR) / CELL) do
      for _, r in ipairs(cells[i * 100003 + j] or {}) do
        if r[6] ~= stamp then
          r[6] = stamp
          local a = math.max(r[1], px - RADAR)
          local b = math.max(r[2], pz - RADAR)
          local c = math.min(r[3], px + RADAR)
          local d = math.min(r[4], pz + RADAR)
          if c > a and d > b then
            local col = r[5]
            engine.rect(x0 + (a - px + RADAR) * k, y0 + (b - pz + RADAR) * k, (c - a) * k, (d - b) * k, col[1], col[2], col[3], 0.9)
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end
  for _, id in ipairs(engine.find("car")) do
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
    if math.abs(x - px) < RADAR and math.abs(z - pz) < RADAR then
      local bx, by = map(x, z)
      engine.rect(bx - 1.5, by - 1.5, 3, 3, 0.4, 0.6, 1.0, 1)
    end
  end
  for _, m in ipairs(G.markers or {}) do
    if engine.exists(m.id) then
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(m.id)
      local bx, by = map(x, z)
      engine.rect(bx - 3, by - 3, 6, 6, m.color[1], m.color[2], m.color[3], 1)
    end
  end
  for _, lm in ipairs(L.labels or {}) do
    if math.abs(lm.x - px) < RADAR and math.abs(lm.z - pz) < RADAR then
      local bx, by = map(lm.x, lm.z)
      engine.rect(bx - 4, by - 4, 8, 8, 1, 1, 1, 1)
      engine.text(lm.letter, bx + 6, by - 6, 10, 1, 1, 1, 1)
    end
  end
  engine.emit("minimap", map)
  engine.rect(x0 + S / 2 - 3, y0 + S / 2 - 3, 6, 6, 1, 1, 1, 1)
end

local function minimap(sw, sh)
  local L = G.city
  if not L then return end
  if L.network and math.max(L.sx, L.sz) > 2 * RADAR * 2.5 then return radar(sw, sh, L) end
  -- The island is tall and thin, so the map is too; it scales with the window.
  local H = math.floor(math.min(230, sh * 0.3))
  local W = math.floor(H * L.sx / L.sz)
  local x0, y0 = sw - W - 20, sh - H - 20
  local function map(wx, wz) return x0 + (wx - (L.cx or 0) + L.sx / 2) / L.sx * W, y0 + (wz - (L.cz or 0) + L.sz / 2) / L.sz * H end
  engine.rect(x0 - 4, y0 - 4, W + 8, H + 8, 0, 0, 0, 0.55)
  engine.rect(x0, y0, W, H, 0.18, 0.32, 0.55, 0.85)
  for _, r in ipairs(map_base(L)) do
    local c = r[5]
    engine.rect(x0 + r[1] * W, y0 + r[2] * H, r[3] * W, r[4] * H, c[1], c[2], c[3], 0.9)
  end
  for _, id in ipairs(engine.find("car")) do
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
    local px, py = map(x, z)
    engine.rect(px - 1.5, py - 1.5, 3, 3, 0.4, 0.6, 1.0, 1)
  end
  for _, m in ipairs(G.markers or {}) do
    if engine.exists(m.id) then
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(m.id)
      local px, py = map(x, z)
      engine.rect(px - 3, py - 3, 6, 6, m.color[1], m.color[2], m.color[3], 1)
    end
  end
  for _, lm in ipairs(L.labels or {}) do
    local px, py = map(lm.x, lm.z)
    engine.rect(px - 4, py - 4, 8, 8, 1, 1, 1, 1)
    engine.text(lm.letter, px + 6, py - 6, 10, 1, 1, 1, 1)
  end
  for _, lm in ipairs(L.landmarks or {}) do
    local px, py = map(lm.x, lm.z)
    engine.rect(px - 4, py - 4, 8, 8, 1, 1, 1, 1)
    engine.text(lm.letter, px + 6, py - 6, 10, 1, 1, 1, 1)
  end
  engine.emit("minimap", map) -- other systems add blips
  local x, _, z = U.player_pos()
  if x then
    local px, py = map(x, z)
    engine.rect(px - 3, py - 3, 6, 6, 1, 1, 1, 1)
  end
end

-- Where you are, on maps drawn as roads: the street and the part of town,
-- shown for a few seconds whenever either changes. A new street has to
-- hold for a moment first, so crossing a junction doesn't flash its name.
local HOLD, SHOW = 0.75, 5
local here = {next = 0}
local function whereabouts(h)
  local L = G.city
  if not (L and L.network) then return end
  local now = engine.time()
  if now >= here.next then
    here.next = now + 0.25
    local x, _, z = U.player_pos()
    if x then
      local street, area = require("lib.roadnet").where(x, z)
      -- Stepping off the road in the same part of town isn't news.
      if not street and area == here.area then street = here.street end
      local key = (street or "") .. "|" .. (area or "")
      if key ~= here.candidate then here.candidate, here.since = key, now end
      if key ~= here.key and now - here.since >= HOLD then
        here.key, here.street, here.area, here.at = key, street, area, now
      end
    end
  end
  if not here.at then return end
  local a = math.min(1, (here.at + SHOW - now) / 1)
  if a <= 0 then return end
  local y = h - (G.vehicle and 150 or 130)
  if here.street then
    engine.text(here.street, 22, y + 2, 22, 0, 0, 0, 0.6 * a)
    engine.text(here.street, 20, y, 22, 1, 1, 1, a)
  end
  if here.area then
    engine.text(here.area, 22, y + 28, 14, 0, 0, 0, 0.6 * a)
    engine.text(here.area, 20, y + 26, 14, 0.95, 0.85, 0.5, a)
  end
end
M.whereabouts = function() return here.street, here.area end

-- Police radio when the wanted level goes up: where you were seen, by name.
local radio
engine.on("wanted_changed", function(level, old)
  local L = G.city
  if not (L and L.network) or level <= old then return end
  local x, _, z = U.player_pos()
  if not x then return end
  local street, area = require("lib.roadnet").where(x, z)
  local at = street and area and (street .. ", " .. area) or street or area
  if not at then return end
  local text = old == 0 and ("DISPATCH: All units, suspect seen on " .. at .. ".")
    or level >= 4 and ("DISPATCH: Every unit to " .. at .. "!")
    or ("DISPATCH: Suspect now on " .. at .. ". Backup on the way.")
  if not street then text = text:gsub(" on ", " in ", 1) end
  radio = {text = text, till = engine.time() + 5}
end)

engine.on("key", function(k, down) if k == "f1" and down then settings.set("help", not settings.get("help")) end end)

engine.on("frame", function(dt)
  local w, h = engine.screen()
  shadow_text(string.format("$%d", G.cash), w - 20 - engine.text_width(string.format("$%d", G.cash), 28), 20, 28, 0.4, 0.9, 0.4)

  -- Clock, under the cash.
  local clock = require("lib.daynight").clock()
  shadow_text(clock, w - 20 - engine.text_width(clock, 14), 92, 14, 0.85, 0.85, 0.85)

  -- Wanted stars; they blink while the cops can't see you.
  local stars = require("lib.wanted")
  if stars.stars() > 0 then
    local show = stars.seen or math.floor(engine.time() * 3) % 2 == 0
    for k = 1, 5 do
      local lit = k <= stars.stars() and show
      local x = w - 20 - (6 - k) * 26
      shadow_text("*", x, 58, 28, lit and 1 or 0.3, lit and 0.85 or 0.3, lit and 0.2 or 0.3)
    end
  end

  local H = require "lib.health"
  if G.vehicle and engine.exists(G.vehicle) then
    local is_boat = engine.tag(G.vehicle) == "boat"
    local kmh = math.abs(require("lib.vehicle").speed(G.vehicle)) * 3.6
    if is_boat then
      local knots = math.floor(kmh * 0.54)
      shadow_text(string.format("%2d KNOTS", knots), 20, h - 50, 28, 0.4, 0.85, 1)
    else
      shadow_text(string.format("%3d KM/H", math.floor(kmh)), 20, h - 50, 28, 1, 1, 1)
    end
    -- Car condition.
    local hp = H.get(G.vehicle) or H.VEHICLE_HP
    bar(20, h - 92, 160, 8, hp / H.VEHICLE_HP, 1, 0.6, 0.1)
  end

  -- Health.
  bar(20, h - 76, 160, 10, H.player.hp / H.player.max, 0.9, 0.15, 0.15)

  -- Weapon, ammo and crosshair (on foot).
  local W = require "systems.weapons"
  local def = W.defs[G.weapon]
  local label = def.name .. (def.melee and "" or string.format("  %d", G.ammo[G.weapon] or 0))
  shadow_text(label, w - 20 - engine.text_width(label, 16), h - math.min(230, h * 0.3) - 55, 16, 1, 1, 1)
  if G.player and not def.melee then
    local cx, cy = w / 2, h / 2
    for _, r in ipairs{{-9, -1, 6, 2}, {3, -1, 6, 2}, {-1, -9, 2, 6}, {-1, 3, 2, 6}} do
      engine.rect(cx + r[1], cy + r[2], r[3], r[4], 1, 1, 1, 0.85)
    end
  end

  -- Wasted: fade to dark red.
  if G.dead then
    local t = math.min(1, (engine.time() - G.dead) / 2)
    engine.rect(0, 0, w, h, 0.25, 0, 0, 0.6 * t)
  end

  local m = G.mission
  if m then
    if m.objective then centered(m.objective, 110, 18, 1, 0.9, 0.5) end
    if m.deadline then
      local left = math.max(0, m.deadline - engine.time())
      centered(string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(left / 60), math.floor(left % 60)), 140, 24, 1, left < 10 and 0.3 or 1, left < 10 and 0.3 or 1)
    end
  end

  if radio and radio.till > engine.time() then centered(radio.text, 170, 14, 0.6, 0.8, 1) end

  -- Big messages, newest last.
  local now, y, keep = engine.time(), h * 0.35, {}
  for _, msg in ipairs(G.messages) do
    if msg.till > now then
      keep[#keep + 1] = msg
      centered(msg.text, y, 30, 1, 0.85, 0.2)
      y = y + 44
    end
  end
  G.messages = keep

  minimap(w, h)
  whereabouts(h)

  if settings.get("help") then
    local lines = require("lib.input").connected() and
      "L STICK MOVE/STEER  R STICK CAMERA  RT ACCELERATE/FIRE  LT BRAKE\nA JUMP/HANDBRAKE  B RUN/HORN  Y ENTER/EXIT CAR  X SHOPS\nLB/RB WEAPONS  BACK PROGRESS  START PAUSE/SETTINGS\nYELLOW MARKERS: MISSIONS  ORANGE RAMPS: STUNT PARK\nF1 HIDE HELP" or "WASD MOVE/DRIVE  SHIFT RUN  SPACE JUMP/HANDBRAKE\nE ENTER/EXIT CAR  H HORN  MOUSE/ARROWS CAMERA\nCLICK/F FIRE  1/2/3/Q WEAPONS\nYELLOW MARKERS: MISSIONS  ORANGE RAMPS: STUNT PARK\nTAB PROGRESS  N SKIP 3 HOURS  F5 RELOAD  F1 HIDE HELP  ESC PAUSE/SETTINGS"
    engine.rect(14, 14, engine.text_width(lines, 12) + 16, 93, 0, 0, 0, 0.45)
    engine.text(lines, 22, 22, 12, 0.9, 0.9, 0.9, 1)
  end
end)

return M

```

--- FILE: game/lib/menu.lua ---
```lua
-- The pause menu. Esc (or minimizing the window) pauses the game: no ticks,
-- so physics, timers and game sounds stop. While paused, input arrives as
-- "menu_key" events, which only this menu listens to.
--
-- Keyboard: up/down (or W/S) to choose, left/right (or A/D) to change a
-- setting, Enter or Space to pick, Esc to go back. The mouse works too.
--
-- Other systems add pages (shops do) with menu.add_page and open them with
-- menu.show(name). An item is {label, act = fn} and may have value = fn
-- returning the text (and an optional {r,g,b}) shown on its right.
local settings = require "lib.settings"
local map_viewer = require "lib.map_viewer"

local M = {open = false, page = "main", sel = 1}

local pages = {
  main = {
    title = "PAUSED",
    items = {
      {label = "RESUME", act = function() M.close() end},
      {label = "SETTINGS", act = function() M.go("settings") end},
      {label = "MAP", act = function() M.go("map") end},
      {label = "CITY", act = function() M.go("city") end, value = function() return string.upper(require("lib.city").map().name) end},
      {label = "QUIT GAME", act = function() engine.quit() end},
    },
  },
  map = {title = "MAP", back = "main", items = {}},
  city = {title = "CITY", back = "main", items = {}},
  settings = {title = "SETTINGS", back = "main", items = {}},
}
for _, s in ipairs(settings.list) do
  table.insert(pages.settings.items, {label = s.label, setting = s.key})
end
table.insert(pages.settings.items, {label = "RESET TO DEFAULTS", act = function() settings.reset() end})
table.insert(pages.settings.items, {label = "BACK", act = function() M.go("main") end})
-- Picking another city starts the game afresh there; cash and missions carry over.
for _, m in ipairs(require("lib.city").MAPS) do
  table.insert(pages.city.items, {label = m[2], act = function()
    local city = require "lib.city"
    if (city.map().id or "") == m[1] then return M.close() end
    city.choose(m[1])
  end, value = function()
    if (require("lib.city").map().id or "") == m[1] then return "HERE", {1, 0.85, 0.2} end
    return ""
  end})
end
table.insert(pages.city.items, {label = "BACK", act = function() M.go("main") end})

local function click() engine.play("ui_click", {ui = true}) end

engine.on("init", function()
  engine.synth("ui_click", {wave = "square", freq = 900, freq_end = 700, dur = 0.04, release = 0.03, volume = 0.18, lowpass = 0.5})
end)

function M.items() return pages[M.page].items end

function M.add_page(name, page) pages[name] = page end

function M.go(page)
  M.page, M.sel = page, 1
  if page == "map" then map_viewer.init() end
end

function M.show(page)
  if M.open then return end
  M.open, M.page, M.sel = true, type(page) == "string" and page or "main", 1
  if M.page == "map" then map_viewer.init() end
  engine.pause(true)
  engine.grab_mouse(false)
  click()
end

function M.close()
  if not M.open then return end
  M.open = false
  engine.pause(false)
  engine.grab_mouse(true)
  click()
end

function M.back()
  local back = pages[M.page].back
  if back then M.go(back) else M.close() end
end

function M.move(d)
  local n = #M.items()
  M.sel = (M.sel - 1 + d) % n + 1
end

-- Activate the selected item, or step its setting (dir = -1 / 1).
function M.press(dir)
  local item = M.items()[M.sel]
  if item.setting then
    settings.nudge(item.setting, dir or 1)
  elseif item.act and not dir then
    item.act()
  else
    return
  end
  click()
end

-- ---- layout and drawing -------------------------------------------------------
local W, ROW, TOP = 420, 34, 74

-- Screen rectangles of the panel and each row, for drawing and clicking.
function M.layout()
  local w, h = engine.screen()
  local items = M.items()
  local ph = TOP + #items * ROW + 46
  local x, y = (w - W) / 2, (h - ph) / 2
  local rows = {}
  for i = 1, #items do rows[i] = {x = x + 14, y = y + TOP + (i - 1) * ROW, w = W - 28, h = ROW - 4} end
  return {x = x, y = y, w = W, h = ph, rows = rows}
end

function M.row_at(px, py)
  for i, r in ipairs(M.layout().rows) do
    if px >= r.x and px < r.x + r.w and py >= r.y and py < r.y + r.h then return i, r end
  end
end

-- A click picks a row. On a setting, clicking the "<" side of its value
-- lowers it and anywhere else raises it (or flips a switch).
function M.click(px, py)
  local i, r = M.row_at(px, py)
  if not i then return end
  M.sel = i
  local item = M.items()[i]
  if item.setting then
    local right = r.x + r.w
    M.press(px >= right - 130 and px < right - 65 and -1 or 1)
  else
    M.press()
  end
end

local last_cursor
engine.on("frame", function()
  if not M.open then return end
  -- Hovering selects, but only when the mouse actually moves.
  local cx, cy = engine.cursor()
  if last_cursor and (cx ~= last_cursor[1] or cy ~= last_cursor[2]) then
    M.sel = M.row_at(cx, cy) or M.sel
  end
  last_cursor = {cx, cy}

  local sw, sh = engine.screen()
  if M.page == "map" then
    map_viewer.draw(sw, sh)
    return
  end

  engine.rect(0, 0, sw, sh, 0, 0, 0, 0.45)
  local L = M.layout()
  engine.rect(L.x, L.y, L.w, L.h, 0.05, 0.06, 0.08, 0.9)
  engine.rect(L.x, L.y, L.w, 4, 1, 0.85, 0.2, 1)
  local title = pages[M.page].title
  if type(title) == "function" then title = title() end
  engine.text(title, L.x + 24, L.y + 26, 24, 1, 0.85, 0.2, 1)
  for i, item in ipairs(M.items()) do
    local r, on = L.rows[i], i == M.sel
    if on then engine.rect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 1, 0.85, 0.2, 0.18) end
    local c = on and 1 or 0.75
    engine.text(item.label, r.x + 12, r.y + 10, 14, c, c, c, 1)
    if item.setting then
      local v = settings.text(item.setting)
      local right = r.x + r.w - 12
      engine.text(v, right - 65 - engine.text_width(v, 14) / 2, r.y + 10, 14, 1, 1, 1, 1)
      if on then
        engine.text("<", right - 118, r.y + 10, 14, 1, 0.85, 0.2, 1)
        engine.text(">", right - engine.text_width(">", 14), r.y + 10, 14, 1, 0.85, 0.2, 1)
      end
    elseif item.value then
      local v, col = item.value()
      col = col or {1, 1, 1}
      engine.text(v, r.x + r.w - 12 - engine.text_width(v, 14), r.y + 10, 14, col[1], col[2], col[3], 1)
    end
  end
  engine.text("ARROWS/WASD CHOOSE  ENTER PICK  ESC BACK", L.x + 24, L.y + L.h - 26, 10, 0.6, 0.6, 0.6, 1)
end)

-- ---- input ----------------------------------------------------------------------
local nav = {up = -1, w = -1, down = 1, s = 1}
local adjust = {left = -1, a = -1, right = 1, d = 1}

engine.on("key", function(k, down)
  if k == "escape" and down then M.show() end
  if k == "m" and down then
    if M.open and M.page == "map" then M.close()
    else M.show("map") end
  end
end)

engine.on("minimized", M.show)

engine.on("menu_key", function(k, down)
  if not down or not M.open then return end
  if M.page == "map" then
    if k == "escape" or k == "m" then
      M.back()
      click()
    elseif k == "mouse1" then
      local cx, cy = engine.cursor()
      local sw, _ = engine.screen()
      if cx >= sw - 120 and cy <= 45 then
        M.back()
        click()
      else
        map_viewer.mouse_down(cx, cy)
      end
    else
      map_viewer.key(k)
    end
    return
  end

  if k == "escape" then
    M.back()
    if M.open then click() end
  elseif nav[k] then M.move(nav[k]); click()
  elseif adjust[k] then M.press(adjust[k])
  elseif k == "enter" or k == "space" then M.press()
  elseif k == "mouse1" then M.click(engine.cursor())
  end
end)

return M

```

--- FILE: game/lib/mission.lua ---
```lua
-- Missions are coroutines. A mission file returns a table:
--
--   return {
--     title = "Name", start = {x, z}, reward = 500,
--     after = "04_getaway",   -- optional: locked until that mission is passed
--     run = function(m)
--       m:say("Get in a car")
--       m:wait(function() return m:in_car() end)
--       local goal = m:marker(100, -40)
--       m:timer(60)
--       m:wait(function() return m:near(goal, 6) end)
--     end,
--   }
--
-- Returning from run() passes the mission; m:fail(reason) or running out of
-- time fails it. Everything spawned through m:spawn/m:marker is cleaned up,
-- handlers added with m:on are removed, and m:defer(fn) runs fn at the end
-- whether the mission passed or failed.
local G = require "lib.state"
local U = require "lib.util"

local M = {defs = {}}
G.markers = {}

local Mission = {}
Mission.__index = Mission

function Mission:say(text) self.objective = text end
-- " on <street>" for a spot on a named street, for objectives; "" if not.
function Mission:on_street(x, z)
  local s = require("lib.city").street(x, z)
  return s and (" on " .. s) or ""
end
function Mission:timer(seconds) self.deadline = engine.time() + seconds end
function Mission:clear_timer() self.deadline = nil end
function Mission:fail(reason) coroutine.yield("fail", reason) end
function Mission:in_car() return G.vehicle ~= nil end
function Mission:wanted(stars) require("lib.wanted").set(stars) end
function Mission:stars() return require("lib.wanted").stars() end

function Mission:on(ev, fn)
  engine.on(ev, fn)
  self:defer(function() engine.off(ev, fn) end)
end

function Mission:defer(fn) self.cleanup[#self.cleanup + 1] = fn end

function Mission:spawn(t)
  local id = engine.spawn(t)
  self.owned[#self.owned + 1] = id
  return id
end

-- A glowing column; returns its entity id.
function Mission:marker(x, z, color)
  color = color or {1, 0.85, 0.1}
  local id = self:spawn{mesh = "marker", pos = {x, 2.5 + U.ground(x, z), z}, scale = 5, color = {color[1], color[2], color[3], 0.35}}
  G.markers[#G.markers + 1] = {id = id, color = color}
  return id
end

function Mission:remove(id)
  engine.despawn(id)
end

function Mission:near(id, radius)
  local x, _, z = U.player_pos()
  local mx, _, mz = engine.pos(id)
  return x and mx and U.dist2d(x, z, mx, mz) <= radius
end

-- Block until pred() is true. The deadline is enforced while waiting.
function Mission:wait(pred)
  while not pred() do coroutine.yield() end
end

function Mission:sleep(seconds)
  local t = engine.time() + seconds
  self:wait(function() return engine.time() >= t end)
end

local function prune_markers()
  local keep = {}
  for _, m in ipairs(G.markers) do if engine.exists(m.id) then keep[#keep + 1] = m end end
  G.markers = keep
end

local function finish(m, passed, reason)
  for _, id in ipairs(m.owned) do engine.despawn(id) end
  for _, fn in ipairs(m.cleanup) do fn() end
  G.mission = nil
  if passed then
    G.cash = G.cash + (m.def.reward or 0)
    m.def.passed = true
    U.message("MISSION PASSED", 4)
    if m.def.reward then U.message(string.format("+$%d", m.def.reward), 4) end
    for _, def in ipairs(M.defs) do
      if def.after == m.def.id and not def.passed then U.message("NEW MISSION: " .. def.title:upper(), 5) end
    end
    engine.emit("mission_passed", m.def)
  else
    U.message("MISSION FAILED", 4)
    if reason then U.message(reason, 4) end
    engine.emit("mission_failed", m.def, reason)
  end
  M.show_start_markers()
end

-- Getting busted or wasted ends whatever you were doing.
engine.on("busted", function()
  if G.mission then finish(G.mission, false, "BUSTED") end
end)
engine.on("wasted", function()
  if G.mission then finish(G.mission, false, "WASTED") end
end)

function M.start(def)
  M.hide_start_markers()
  local m = setmetatable({def = def, owned = {}, cleanup = {}}, Mission)
  m.co = coroutine.create(function() def.run(m) return "pass" end)
  G.mission = m
  U.message(def.title:upper(), 3)
  engine.emit("mission_started", def)
end

engine.on("tick", function()
  local m = G.mission
  if not m then
    -- Walk or drive into a start marker to begin.
    for _, def in ipairs(M.defs) do
      if def.marker and engine.exists(def.marker) then
        local x, _, z = U.player_pos()
        if x and U.dist2d(x, z, def.start[1], def.start[2]) < 3 then return M.start(def) end
      end
    end
    return
  end
  if m.deadline and engine.time() > m.deadline then return finish(m, false, "OUT OF TIME") end
  local ok, status, reason = coroutine.resume(m.co)
  if not ok then
    finish(m, false, "SCRIPT ERROR")
    error(debug.traceback(m.co, status), 0)
  elseif status == "pass" then finish(m, true)
  elseif status == "fail" then finish(m, false, reason) end
  prune_markers()
end)

-- A mission with `after` waits for that one to be passed.
function M.unlocked(def)
  return def.after == nil or (G.passed or {})[def.after] == true
end

function M.show_start_markers()
  M.hide_start_markers()
  for _, def in ipairs(M.defs) do
    if M.unlocked(def) then
      local c = def.passed and {0.3, 1, 0.3} or {1, 0.85, 0.1}
      def.marker = engine.spawn{mesh = "marker", pos = {def.start[1], 1.5 + U.ground(def.start[1], def.start[2]), def.start[2]}, scale = {2.5, 3, 2.5}, color = {c[1], c[2], c[3], 0.45}}
      G.markers[#G.markers + 1] = {id = def.marker, color = c}
    end
  end
end

function M.hide_start_markers()
  for _, def in ipairs(M.defs) do
    if def.marker then engine.despawn(def.marker) end
    def.marker = nil
  end
  prune_markers()
end

-- Add a mission. Mods call this; the game's own missions come from load_all.
function M.register(def)
  assert(type(def) == "table" and def.title and def.start and def.run, "a mission needs title, start and run")
  def.id = def.id or def.title
  if G.passed[def.id] then def.passed = true end
  M.defs[#M.defs + 1] = def
  if M.ready then M.show_start_markers() end
end

function M.all() return M.defs end

-- Every .lua file in the directory is a mission. Drop one in, press F5.
function M.load_all(dir)
  -- A unit cylinder that glows at night.
  engine.mesh("marker", {{mesh = "cylinder", emissive = 1}})
  for _, f in ipairs(engine.files(dir)) do
    local name = f:match("^(.+)%.lua$")
    if name then
      local def = require(dir .. "." .. name)
      -- A mission returns false when the map lacks a place it needs.
      if type(def) == "table" then
        def.id = name
        M.register(def)
      else
        print("mission " .. name .. " is not on this map")
      end
    end
  end
  M.ready = true
  M.show_start_markers()
  print(string.format("loaded %d missions", #M.defs))
end

return M

```

--- FILE: game/lib/borders.lua ---
```lua
-- Municipal borders, border crossings, natural barriers and restricted military zones.
-- OpenStreetMap data (ODbL) + terrain elevation (Maanmittauslaitos CC BY 4.0).
local G = require "lib.state"
local U = require "lib.util"
local wanted = require "lib.wanted"

local B = {
  fences = {},
  gates = {},
  crossings = {},
  military_zones = {},
}

function B.build(map)
  if not map then return end
  local roadnet = require "lib.roadnet"

  -- 1. Military zone perimeter fences, gates, and guard posts (e.g. Pansion varuskunta)
  for _, m in ipairs(map.military or {}) do
    local ring = m.polygon
    if ring and #ring >= 3 then
      B.military_zones[#B.military_zones + 1] = {
        name = m.name or "Military Area",
        stars = m.stars or 3,
        polygon = ring,
      }

      -- Spawn security fences along segments, skipping gate openings
      local gates = m.gates or {}
      for i = 2, #ring do
        local ax, az = ring[i - 1][1], ring[i - 1][2]
        local bx, bz = ring[i][1], ring[i][2]
        local len = U.dist2d(ax, az, bx, bz)
        if len > 0.1 then
          local yaw = math.atan(bx - ax, bz - az)
          local n = math.max(1, math.ceil(len / 4))
          for k = 0, n - 1 do
            local mx = ax + (bx - ax) * (k + 0.5) / n
            local mz = az + (bz - az) * (k + 0.5) / n
            -- Check if near any gate opening
            local near_gate = false
            for _, g in ipairs(gates) do
              if U.dist2d(mx, mz, g.pos[1], g.pos[2]) < (g.width or 7) / 2 + 1 then
                near_gate = true
                break
              end
            end
            if not near_gate then
              local ground_y = U.ground(mx, mz)
              -- Extrude fence panel with posts
              local seg_len = len / n
              engine.spawn{
                mesh = "cube",
                pos = {mx, ground_y + 1.25, mz},
                yaw = yaw,
                size = {0.12, 2.5, seg_len * 1.02},
                color = {0.32, 0.35, 0.34},
                tag = "fence",
                body = "static",
              }
              -- Barbed-wire top strip
              engine.spawn{
                mesh = "cube",
                pos = {mx, ground_y + 2.55, mz},
                yaw = yaw,
                size = {0.35, 0.15, seg_len * 1.02},
                color = {0.22, 0.22, 0.25},
                tag = "fence",
                body = "static",
              }
            end
          end
        end
      end

      -- Spawn gates with guardhouse, barrier posts, and boom barriers
      for _, g in ipairs(gates) do
        local gx, gz = g.pos[1], g.pos[2]
        local gy = U.ground(gx, gz)
        local gyaw = g.yaw or 0
        local rx, rz = U.right(gyaw)

        -- Guardhouse prop
        engine.spawn{
          mesh = "guardhouse",
          pos = {gx + rx * 4.2, gy, gz + rz * 4.2},
          yaw = gyaw,
          tag = "guardhouse",
          body = "static",
        }

        -- Barrier posts on either side
        engine.spawn{
          mesh = "barrier_post",
          pos = {gx - rx * 2.8, gy, gz - rz * 2.8},
          yaw = gyaw,
          tag = "prop",
          body = "static",
        }
        engine.spawn{
          mesh = "barrier_post",
          pos = {gx + rx * 2.8, gy, gz + rz * 2.8},
          yaw = gyaw,
          tag = "prop",
          body = "static",
        }

        -- Boom barrier arm across the entrance
        local arm_id = engine.spawn{
          mesh = "cube",
          pos = {gx, gy + 1.1, gz},
          yaw = gyaw,
          size = {5.4, 0.14, 0.14},
          color = {0.95, 0.2, 0.15},
          tag = "boom_barrier",
        }

        B.gates[#B.gates + 1] = {
          pos = {gx, gz},
          radius = 9,
          barrier_id = arm_id,
          open = false,
          arm_y = gy + 1.1,
          yaw = gyaw,
        }
      end
    end
  end

  -- 2. Wooden footbridge over river/boundary (e.g. Punttamäenpolun silta)
  for _, fb in ipairs(map.wooden_footbridges or {}) do
    local width = fb.width or 2.6
    local pts = fb.points
    if pts and #pts >= 2 then
      local total_len = 0
      for i = 2, #pts do total_len = total_len + U.dist2d(pts[i-1][1], pts[i-1][2], pts[i][1], pts[i][2]) end
      for i = 2, #pts do
        local ax, az = pts[i - 1][1], pts[i - 1][2]
        local bx, bz = pts[i][1], pts[i][2]
        local seg = U.dist2d(ax, az, bx, bz)
        local n = math.max(1, math.ceil(seg / 3))
        local yaw = math.atan(bx - ax, bz - az)
        local rx, rz = U.right(yaw)
        for k = 0, n - 1 do
          local t0, t1 = k / n, (k + 1) / n
          local mx = ax + (bx - ax) * (t0 + t1) / 2
          local mz = az + (bz - az) * (t0 + t1) / 2
          local my = math.max(U.ground(mx, mz), 1.6)
          -- Wooden deck planks
          engine.spawn{
            mesh = "cube",
            pos = {mx, my + 0.1, mz},
            yaw = yaw,
            size = {width, 0.2, seg / n * 1.02},
            color = {0.48, 0.36, 0.24}, -- warm wood plank color
            tag = "bridge",
            body = "static",
          }
          -- Wooden handrails on left and right
          for _, side in ipairs{-1, 1} do
            engine.spawn{
              mesh = "cube",
              pos = {mx + rx * side * (width / 2 - 0.08), my + 0.65, mz + rz * side * (width / 2 - 0.08)},
              yaw = yaw,
              size = {0.14, 0.9, seg / n * 1.02},
              color = {0.42, 0.31, 0.19},
              tag = "wall",
              body = "static",
            }
          end
        end
        -- Wooden bollards at both bridge ends so cars cannot physically enter
        for _, end_pt in ipairs{pts[1], pts[#pts]} do
          local ey = U.ground(end_pt[1], end_pt[2])
          engine.spawn{
            mesh = "wood_bollard",
            pos = {end_pt[1], ey, end_pt[2]},
            tag = "bollard",
            body = "static",
          }
        end
      end
    end
  end

  -- 3. Border road crossings with roadworks/blockades
  for _, c in ipairs(map.border_crossings or {}) do
    B.crossings[#B.crossings + 1] = {
      pos = c.pos,
      target_map = c.target_map or "turku",
      target_pos = c.target_pos or {0, 0},
      name = c.name,
    }
    local cx, cz = c.pos[1], c.pos[2]
    local cy = U.ground(cx, cz)
    local cyaw = c.yaw or 0
    local rx, rz = U.right(cyaw)
    local width = c.kind == "highway" and 12 or 9

    -- Place roadside boundary markers and warning cones (leaving drivable passing lane)
    local count = math.ceil(width / 3.0)
    for i = 0, count - 1 do
      local offset = (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 2.8
      engine.spawn{
        mesh = "jersey_barrier",
        pos = {cx + rx * offset, cy, cz + rz * offset},
        tag = "barrier",
        body = "static",
      }
    end
  end

  for _, c in ipairs(map.crossings or {}) do
    B.crossings[#B.crossings + 1] = {
      pos = c.pos,
      target_map = c.target_map or "raisio",
      target_pos = c.target_pos or {0, 0},
      name = c.name,
    }
    local cx, cz = c.pos[1], c.pos[2]
    local cy = U.ground(cx, cz)
    local cyaw = c.yaw or 0
    local rx, rz = U.right(cyaw)
    engine.spawn{
      mesh = "jersey_barrier",
      pos = {cx + rx * 2.5, cy, cz + rz * 2.5},
      tag = "barrier",
      body = "static",
    }
    engine.spawn{
      mesh = "jersey_barrier",
      pos = {cx - rx * 2.5, cy, cz - rz * 2.5},
      tag = "barrier",
      body = "static",
    }
  end
end

-- Tick checks: wanted level alert on crossing military perimeter & gate boom barrier interaction
local warned = false
local last_check = 0
local transitioning = false

engine.on("tick", function()
  local px, py, pz = U.player_pos()
  if not px then return end

  -- Check military zone perimeter intrusion
  for _, zone in ipairs(B.military_zones) do
    if U.inside_polygon(px, pz, zone.polygon) then
      if wanted.stars() < zone.stars then
        wanted.set(zone.stars)
        if not warned then
          U.message("RESTRICTED MILITARY ZONE: " .. zone.name, 4)
          warned = true
        end
      end
    else
      warned = false
    end
  end

  local now = engine.time()
  if now - last_check < 0.25 then return end
  last_check = now

  -- 4. Check seamless cross-border highway transitions
  if not transitioning and (B.crossings and #B.crossings > 0) then
    for _, cr in ipairs(B.crossings) do
      if U.dist2d(px, pz, cr.pos[1], cr.pos[2]) < 12 then
        transitioning = true
        U.message("ENTERING " .. (cr.target_map == "turku" and "TURKU" or "RAISIO") .. "...", 3)
        -- Save player transition state: car/on-foot, wanted level, weapons, cash
        local v_id = G.vehicle
        local state = {
          target_pos = cr.target_pos or {0, 0},
          wanted_stars = wanted.stars(),
          in_vehicle = (v_id ~= nil),
        }
        engine.save("cross_city_transfer", state)
        local city = require "lib.city"
        city.choose(cr.target_map)
        return
      end
    end
  end

  -- Check gate approach: if wanted, gate stays down / alarms; if player approaches gate, raise boom barrier
  for _, g in ipairs(B.gates) do
    local d = U.dist2d(px, pz, g.pos[1], g.pos[2])
    if d < g.radius and not g.open then
      -- Animate/raise barrier
      g.open = true
      engine.set_pos(g.barrier_id, g.pos[1], g.arm_y + 3.0, g.pos[2])
    elseif d >= g.radius + 3 and g.open then
      -- Lower barrier
      g.open = false
      engine.set_pos(g.barrier_id, g.pos[1], g.arm_y, g.pos[2])
    end
  end
end)

return B

```

--- FILE: game/lib/map_viewer.lua ---
```lua
-- Fullscreen interactive vector map viewer for pause menu and navigation.
local U = require "lib.util"
local G = require "lib.state"
local city = require "lib.city"

local V = {
  pan_x = 0,
  pan_z = 0,
  zoom = 0.35,
  inited = false,
  dragging = false,
  drag_start_x = 0,
  drag_start_y = 0,
  drag_pan_x = 0,
  drag_pan_z = 0,
}

local COLORS = {
  bg = {0.05, 0.06, 0.09},
  viewport = {0.12, 0.15, 0.19},
  road = {0.28, 0.30, 0.34},
  arterial = {0.44, 0.46, 0.52},
  water = {0.16, 0.28, 0.36},
  accent = {1.0, 0.85, 0.2},
}

function V.recenter()
  local px, _, pz = U.player_pos()
  local L = G.city
  if px then
    V.pan_x = px
    V.pan_z = pz
  elseif L then
    V.pan_x = L.cx or 0
    V.pan_z = L.cz or 0
  else
    V.pan_x, V.pan_z = 0, 0
  end
end

function V.init()
  V.recenter()
  local L = G.city
  local sw, sh = engine.screen()
  local vw, vh = sw - 40, sh - 116
  if L and L.sx and L.sz then
    V.zoom = math.max(0.12, math.min(1.2, math.min(vw / L.sx, vh / L.sz) * 1.5))
  else
    V.zoom = 0.35
  end
  V.inited = true
end

function V.key(k)
  local speed = 140 / math.max(0.05, V.zoom)
  if k == "w" or k == "up" then V.pan_z = V.pan_z - speed
  elseif k == "s" or k == "down" then V.pan_z = V.pan_z + speed
  elseif k == "a" or k == "left" then V.pan_x = V.pan_x - speed
  elseif k == "d" or k == "right" then V.pan_x = V.pan_x + speed
  elseif k == "=" or k == "+" or k == "e" then V.zoom = math.min(1.8, V.zoom * 1.25)
  elseif k == "-" or k == "_" or k == "q" then V.zoom = math.max(0.04, V.zoom / 1.25)
  elseif k == "space" or k == "enter" then V.recenter()
  end
end

function V.mouse_down(x, y)
  V.dragging = true
  V.drag_start_x = x
  V.drag_start_y = y
  V.drag_pan_x = V.pan_x
  V.drag_pan_z = V.pan_z
end

function V.mouse_up()
  V.dragging = false
end

function V.frame()
  if V.dragging then
    local cx, cy = engine.cursor()
    V.pan_x = V.drag_pan_x - (cx - V.drag_start_x) / math.max(0.01, V.zoom)
    V.pan_z = V.drag_pan_z - (cy - V.drag_start_y) / math.max(0.01, V.zoom)
    if not engine.key("mouse1") then
      V.dragging = false
    end
  end
end

function V.draw(sw, sh)
  sw, sh = sw or 960, sh or 540
  V.frame()
  local cur_map = city.map()
  local L = G.city

  -- 1. Fullscreen dark backdrop
  engine.rect(0, 0, sw, sh, COLORS.bg[1], COLORS.bg[2], COLORS.bg[3], 0.98)

  -- 2. Top Header
  engine.rect(0, 0, sw, 52, 0.08, 0.09, 0.12, 1)
  engine.rect(0, 52, sw, 2, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)
  local title = string.upper(cur_map.name or "CITY") .. " — SATELLITE MAP"
  engine.text(title, 24, 16, 20, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)

  -- Top right: current street / area or back button
  local px, py, pz = U.player_pos()
  if px then
    local street, area
    if L and L.network then
      street, area = require("lib.roadnet").where(px, pz)
    end
    local loc_str = (street or "DISTRICT") .. (area and (" (" .. area .. ")") or "")
    local tw = engine.text_width(string.upper(loc_str), 12)
    engine.text(string.upper(loc_str), sw - 140 - tw, 20, 12, 0.8, 0.82, 0.85, 1)
  end

  engine.rect(sw - 110, 12, 88, 28, 0.15, 0.18, 0.22, 1)
  engine.text("ESC: BACK", sw - 98, 19, 12, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)

  -- 3. Viewport setup
  local vx, vy = 20, 64
  local vw, vh = sw - 40, sh - 116
  engine.rect(vx, vy, vw, vh, COLORS.viewport[1], COLORS.viewport[2], COLORS.viewport[3], 1)

  local function to_screen(wx, wz)
    local sx = vx + vw / 2 + (wx - V.pan_x) * V.zoom
    local sy = vy + vh / 2 + (wz - V.pan_z) * V.zoom
    return sx, sy
  end

  local function in_view(sx, sy, pad)
    pad = pad or 12
    return sx >= vx - pad and sx <= vx + vw + pad and sy >= vy - pad and sy <= vy + vh + pad
  end

  -- 4. Water areas
  local cover = cur_map.cover
  if cover and cover.water then
    for _, st in ipairs(cover.water) do
      local s1x, s1y = to_screen(st[1], st[2])
      local s2x, s2y = to_screen(st[3], st[4])
      local rx, ry = math.min(s1x, s2x), math.min(s1y, s2y)
      local rw, rh = math.abs(s2x - s1x), math.abs(s2y - s1y)
      local cx = math.max(vx, rx)
      local cy = math.max(vy, ry)
      local cw = math.min(vx + vw, rx + rw) - cx
      local ch = math.min(vy + vh, ry + rh) - cy
      if cw > 0 and ch > 0 then
        engine.rect(cx, cy, cw, ch, COLORS.water[1], COLORS.water[2], COLORS.water[3], 0.95)
      end
    end
  end

  -- 5. Aurajoki River corridor if in Turku
  if cur_map.id == "turku" then
    local river_pts = {
      {450, -320}, {280, -180}, {120, -70}, {-60, 40},
      {-280, 180}, {-520, 340}, {-800, 520}, {-1200, 780}, {-1600, 1050}
    }
    local rsize = math.max(8, 46 * V.zoom)
    for i = 2, #river_pts do
      local p0, p1 = river_pts[i - 1], river_pts[i]
      local ax, ay = to_screen(p0[1], p0[2])
      local bx, by = to_screen(p1[1], p1[2])
      local dist = math.sqrt((bx - ax)^2 + (by - ay)^2)
      local steps = math.max(1, math.floor(dist / 8))
      for k = 0, steps do
        local rx = ax + (bx - ax) * k / steps
        local ry = ay + (by - ay) * k / steps
        if in_view(rx, ry, rsize) then
          local cx = math.max(vx, rx - rsize / 2)
          local cy = math.max(vy, ry - rsize / 2)
          local cw = math.min(vx + vw, rx + rsize / 2) - cx
          local ch = math.min(vy + vh, ry + rsize / 2) - cy
          if cw > 0 and ch > 0 then
            engine.rect(cx, cy, cw, ch, COLORS.water[1], COLORS.water[2], COLORS.water[3], 0.95)
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  -- 6. Road network
  if L and L.network then
    local net = require("lib.roadnet").net()
    for _, e in ipairs(net.edges) do
      local rw = math.max(2, (e.width or 12) * V.zoom)
      local is_arterial = (e.width and e.width >= 16) or e.oneway
      local rc = is_arterial and COLORS.arterial or COLORS.road
      for i = 2, #e.pts do
        local p0, p1 = e.pts[i - 1], e.pts[i]
        local ax, ay = to_screen(p0[1], p0[2])
        local bx, by = to_screen(p1[1], p1[2])
        if in_view(ax, ay, 20) or in_view(bx, by, 20) then
          local dist = math.sqrt((bx - ax)^2 + (by - ay)^2)
          local steps = math.max(1, math.floor(dist / math.max(3, rw * 0.7)))
          for k = 0, steps do
            local rx = ax + (bx - ax) * k / steps
            local ry = ay + (by - ay) * k / steps
            if rx >= vx and rx + rw <= vx + vw and ry >= vy and ry + rw <= vy + vh then
              engine.rect(rx - rw / 2, ry - rw / 2, rw, rw, rc[1], rc[2], rc[3], 0.95)
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  elseif L then
    local A, S = (L.avenue or 16) / 2, (L.street or 13) / 2
    for _, j in ipairs(city.junctions()) do
      local jx, jz = city.pos(j)
      local sx, sy = to_screen(jx, jz)
      local jw = math.max(3, A * 2 * V.zoom)
      if in_view(sx, sy, 10) then
        engine.rect(sx - jw / 2, sy - jw / 2, jw, jw, COLORS.road[1], COLORS.road[2], COLORS.road[3], 0.95)
      end
    end
    for _, r in ipairs(city.roads()) do
      local ax, az = city.pos(r[1])
      local bx, bz = city.pos(r[2])
      local asx, asy = to_screen(ax, az)
      local bsx, bsy = to_screen(bx, bz)
      local rx, ry = math.min(asx, bsx), math.min(asy, bsy)
      local rw = math.max(math.abs(bsx - asx), math.max(2, A * 2 * V.zoom))
      local rh = math.max(math.abs(bsy - asy), math.max(2, S * 2 * V.zoom))
      if in_view(rx, ry, 20) then
        engine.rect(rx, ry, rw, rh, COLORS.road[1], COLORS.road[2], COLORS.road[3], 0.95)
      end
    end
  end

  -- 7. Ambient Traffic
  for _, id in ipairs(engine.find("car")) do
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
    local cx, cy = to_screen(x, z)
    if in_view(cx, cy, 4) then
      engine.rect(cx - 2, cy - 2, 4, 4, 0.35, 0.65, 0.95, 0.85)
    end
  end

  -- 8. Mission Objectives
  for _, m in ipairs(G.markers or {}) do
    if engine.exists(m.id) then
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(m.id)
      local mx, my = to_screen(x, z)
      if in_view(mx, my, 12) then
        local col = m.color or {0.9, 0.8, 0.2}
        engine.rect(mx - 6, my - 6, 12, 12, col[1], col[2], col[3], 1)
        engine.rect(mx - 8, my - 8, 16, 2, col[1], col[2], col[3], 0.8)
      end
    end
  end

  -- 9. Landmarks and Labels
  if L then
    for _, lm in ipairs(L.labels or {}) do
      local lx, ly = to_screen(lm.x, lm.z)
      if in_view(lx, ly, 30) then
        engine.rect(lx - 5, ly - 5, 10, 10, 1, 1, 1, 1)
        engine.text(lm.letter or "*", lx - 3, ly - 5, 10, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 1)
        engine.text(lm.name, lx + 9, ly - 5, 11, 0.95, 0.95, 0.95, 1)
      end
    end
    for _, lm in ipairs(L.landmarks or {}) do
      local lx, ly = to_screen(lm.x, lm.z)
      if in_view(lx, ly, 30) then
        engine.rect(lx - 5, ly - 5, 10, 10, 1, 1, 1, 1)
        engine.text(lm.letter or "*", lx - 3, ly - 5, 10, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 1)
        engine.text(lm.name, lx + 9, ly - 5, 11, 0.95, 0.95, 0.95, 1)
      end
    end
  end

  -- 10. Player Icon & Heading
  if px then
    local psx, psy = to_screen(px, pz)
    if in_view(psx, psy, 12) then
      engine.rect(psx - 6, psy - 6, 12, 12, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)
      engine.rect(psx - 4, psy - 4, 8, 8, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 1)
      local yaw = U.player_yaw()
      if yaw then
        local fx, fz = U.forward(yaw)
        engine.rect(psx + fx * 12 - 2, psy + fz * 12 - 2, 4, 4, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)
      end
      engine.text("YOU", psx + 10, psy - 5, 11, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)
    end
  end

  -- Viewport Outer Border
  engine.rect(vx, vy, vw, 2, 0.35, 0.40, 0.48, 1)
  engine.rect(vx, vy + vh - 2, vw, 2, 0.35, 0.40, 0.48, 1)
  engine.rect(vx, vy, 2, vh, 0.35, 0.40, 0.48, 1)
  engine.rect(vx + vw - 2, vy, 2, vh, 0.35, 0.40, 0.48, 1)

  -- 11. Bottom Controls Footer
  engine.rect(0, sh - 48, sw, 48, 0.08, 0.09, 0.12, 1)
  engine.rect(0, sh - 48, sw, 1, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30, 1)
  engine.text("WASD / ARROWS: PAN    +/-: ZOOM    SPACE: RECENTER    ESC: RETURN", 24, sh - 30, 11, 0.75, 0.75, 0.75, 1)
  local zoom_pct = string.format("ZOOM: %d%%", math.floor(V.zoom / 0.35 * 100))
  engine.text(zoom_pct, sw - 24 - engine.text_width(zoom_pct, 11), sh - 30, 11, COLORS.accent[1], COLORS.accent[2], COLORS.accent[3], 1)
end

return V

```

--- FILE: game/systems/peds.lua ---
```lua
-- Pedestrians: walk the sidewalk loop around their block (or, on a map
-- drawn as roads, along the pavements from junction to junction),
-- sometimes cross the street at a corner, run from danger, and get knocked flying by cars.
-- Pure Lua on the engine API; the population follows the player around.
local G = require "lib.state"
local U = require "lib.util"
local city = require "lib.city"
local prefabs = require "lib.prefabs"
local H = require "lib.health"

local Ped = {peds = {}, down = {}}
local COUNT, WALK, RUN = 40, 1.4, 6.5
local SKINS = {{0.95, 0.8, 0.65}, {0.85, 0.65, 0.5}, {0.6, 0.42, 0.3}, {0.4, 0.28, 0.2}}
local SHIRTS = {{0.8, 0.2, 0.2}, {0.2, 0.4, 0.8}, {0.9, 0.8, 0.3}, {0.3, 0.6, 0.35}, {0.15, 0.15, 0.15}, {0.9, 0.9, 0.9}, {0.6, 0.3, 0.6}, {0.9, 0.5, 0.2}}
local PANTS = {{0.15, 0.2, 0.4}, {0.25, 0.25, 0.25}, {0.5, 0.4, 0.3}, {0.1, 0.1, 0.1}}
local HAIR = {{0.1, 0.07, 0.04}, {0.45, 0.3, 0.12}, {0.8, 0.7, 0.4}, {0.5, 0.5, 0.5}}
local VARIANTS = 12

function Ped.build()
  local function pick(t, i) return t[i % #t + 1] end
  for i = 1, VARIANTS do
    prefabs.person("ped" .. i, pick(SHIRTS, i), pick(PANTS, i * 3), pick(SKINS, i * 5), pick(HAIR, i * 7))
  end
  engine.synth("scream", {wave = "square", freq = 780, freq_end = 420, dur = 0.5, attack = 0.03, release = 0.25, volume = 0.18, lowpass = 0.25})
end

-- ---- sidewalk geometry ------------------------------------------------------

-- Corners counter-clockwise; index <-> (sx, sz) sign pair.
local SIGNS = {{1, 1}, {-1, 1}, {-1, -1}, {1, -1}}

local function corner(i, j, k)
  local cx, cz = city.block_center(i, j)
  return cx + SIGNS[k][1] * (G.city.bx / 2 - 2.2), cz + SIGNS[k][2] * (G.city.bz / 2 - 2.2)
end

local block_of = city.block_of
local function walkable(i, j) return city.district(i, j) ~= "water" end

local function nearest_corner(p, x, z)
  local best, bk = math.huge, 1
  for k = 1, 4 do
    local cx, cz = corner(p.i, p.j, k)
    local d = U.dist2d(x, z, cx, cz)
    if d < best then best, bk = d, k end
  end
  return bk
end

local function set_target(p, x, z) p.tx, p.tz = x, z end

-- Reached a corner: keep circling, or now and then cross to the next block.
local function next_leg(p)
  local s = SIGNS[p.k]
  if math.random() < 0.25 then
    local opts = {}
    if walkable(p.i + s[1], p.j) then opts[#opts + 1] = {p.i + s[1], p.j, -s[1], s[2]} end
    if walkable(p.i, p.j + s[2]) then opts[#opts + 1] = {p.i, p.j + s[2], s[1], -s[2]} end
    if #opts > 0 then
      local o = opts[math.random(#opts)]
      for k, sg in ipairs(SIGNS) do
        if sg[1] == o[3] and sg[2] == o[4] then p.i, p.j, p.k = o[1], o[2], k end
      end
      p.crossing = true
      return set_target(p, corner(p.i, p.j, p.k))
    end
  end
  p.crossing = false
  p.k = (p.k - 1 + p.dir) % 4 + 1
  set_target(p, corner(p.i, p.j, p.k))
end

-- ---- pavements of a map drawn as roads ---------------------------------------
-- There a ped walks the pavement along one road (edge e), toward bend wp,
-- in direction dir (+1 up the bends, -1 down), on the `hand` side of the
-- way it's walking. At a junction it takes another road there, now and
-- then crossing to the other side.

local roadnet = require "lib.roadnet"
local net = {}

local function edges() return roadnet.net().edges end

-- Keep ped p on a clear pavement of its road: its side of the road is
-- hand * dir; switch hands if that side's pavement isn't walkable.
local function clear_side(p)
  if not roadnet.pavement(p.e, p.hand * p.dir) then p.hand = -p.hand end
end

-- Walkable roads meeting at each junction.
local function at_junction(j)
  if not net.inc then
    net.inc = {}
    for ei, e in ipairs(edges()) do
      if roadnet.walkable(ei) then
        for _, end_ in ipairs{e.a, e.b} do
          net.inc[end_] = net.inc[end_] or {}
          table.insert(net.inc[end_], ei)
        end
      end
    end
  end
  return net.inc[j] or {}
end

-- Bend i of the ped's road, moved out to the pavement on its hand side.
function net.point(p, i)
  local e = edges()[p.e]
  local pts = e.pts
  local j = (pts[i - p.dir] and i - p.dir) or i + p.dir -- a neighbor to take the direction from
  local a, b = pts[math.min(i, j)], pts[math.max(i, j)]
  local fx, fz = b[1] - a[1], b[2] - a[2]
  if p.dir < 0 then fx, fz = -fx, -fz end
  local l = math.sqrt(fx * fx + fz * fz) + 1e-6
  local o = (e.width / 2 + roadnet.SIDEWALK / 2) * p.hand
  return pts[i][1] + fz / l * o, pts[i][2] - fx / l * o
end

function net.next_leg(p)
  local pts = edges()[p.e].pts
  local n = p.wp + p.dir
  p.crossing = false
  if n >= 1 and n <= #pts then
    p.wp = n
  else
    -- At a junction: carry on down another road (back the way we came only
    -- at a dead end), now and then crossing over.
    local e = edges()[p.e]
    local j = p.dir > 0 and e.b or e.a
    local opts = {}
    for _, ei in ipairs(at_junction(j)) do if ei ~= p.e then opts[#opts + 1] = ei end end
    local ne = opts[math.random(math.max(1, #opts))] or p.e
    local hand = p.hand
    -- Only cross over at junctions if both sides of the new road have walkable pavements
    local ed = edges()[ne]
    local both_sides_walkable = roadnet.pavement(ne, 1) and roadnet.pavement(ne, -1)
    if both_sides_walkable and math.random() < 0.25 then
      p.hand = -p.hand
    end
    p.e = ne
    if ed.a == j then p.dir, p.wp = 1, 1 else p.dir, p.wp = -1, #ed.pts end
    clear_side(p)
    p.crossing = p.hand ~= hand
  end
  set_target(p, net.point(p, p.wp))
end

-- Back to the nearest pavement (after running away).
function net.rejoin(p, x, z)
  local e, i = roadnet.nearest_edge(x, z, true)
  local pts = edges()[e].pts
  local a, b = pts[i - 1], pts[i]
  local side = (b[1] - a[1]) * (z - a[2]) - (b[2] - a[2]) * (x - a[1]) -- > 0: left of a->b
  p.e, p.dir = e, math.random() < 0.5 and 1 or -1
  p.wp = p.dir > 0 and i or i - 1
  p.hand = (side > 0) == (p.dir > 0) and -1 or 1
  clear_side(p)
  set_target(p, net.point(p, p.wp))
end

function net.turn(p)
  p.dir, p.hand = -p.dir, -p.hand
  local pts = edges()[p.e].pts
  p.wp = math.max(1, math.min(#pts, p.wp + p.dir))
  set_target(p, net.point(p, p.wp))
end

function net.spawn(near_x, near_z, min_d, max_d)
  -- Only roads that come within reach are worth trying.
  local E, near = edges(), {}
  for ei, e in ipairs(E) do
    if roadnet.walkable(ei) then
      for _, q in ipairs(e.pts) do
        if U.dist2d(q[1], q[2], near_x, near_z) < max_d + 60 then near[#near + 1] = ei break end
      end
    end
  end
  if #near == 0 then return end
  for _ = 1, 30 do
    local ei = near[math.random(#near)]
    local pts = E[ei].pts
    local i = math.random(2, #pts)
    do
      local p = {e = ei, dir = math.random() < 0.5 and 1 or -1, hand = math.random() < 0.5 and 1 or -1}
      clear_side(p)
      p.wp = p.dir > 0 and i or i - 1
      local ax, az = net.point(p, p.dir > 0 and i - 1 or i)
      local bx, bz = net.point(p, p.wp)
      local t = math.random()
      local x, z = ax + (bx - ax) * t, az + (bz - az) * t
      local dist = U.dist2d(x, z, near_x, near_z)
      if dist >= min_d and dist <= max_d and not engine.nearest(x, 1, z, 1.5) then return p, x, z end
    end
  end
end

-- ---- population ---------------------------------------------------------------

local function spawn_ped(near_x, near_z, min_d, max_d)
  if city.network() then
    local p, x, z = net.spawn(near_x, near_z, min_d, max_d)
    if not p then return end
    local id = engine.spawn{mesh = "ped" .. math.random(VARIANTS), pos = {x, 1.15 + U.ground(x, z), z}, size = {0.7, 1.8, 0.7},
                            body = "dynamic", mass = 70, friction = 0, step = 0.45, lock_rotation = true, tag = "ped"}
    H.set(id, 30)
    p.id, p.speed, p.state, p.stuck = id, WALK * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.4), "walk", 0
    set_target(p, net.point(p, p.wp))
    Ped.peds[id] = p
    return id
  end
  local bi, bj = block_of(near_x, near_z)
  for _ = 1, 30 do
    -- A block around the player: avenues are twice as far apart as streets.
    local i, j = bi + math.random(-2, 2), bj + math.random(-4, 4)
    if not walkable(i, j) then goto continue end
    local k = math.random(4)
    local a, b = corner(i, j, k)
    local n = k % 4 + 1
    local c, d = corner(i, j, n)
    local t = math.random()
    local x, z = a + (c - a) * t, b + (d - b) * t
    local dist = U.dist2d(x, z, near_x, near_z)
    if dist >= min_d and dist <= max_d and not engine.nearest(x, 1, z, 1.5) then
      local id = engine.spawn{mesh = "ped" .. math.random(VARIANTS), pos = {x, 1.15 + U.ground(x, z), z}, size = {0.7, 1.8, 0.7},
                              body = "dynamic", mass = 70, friction = 0, step = 0.45, lock_rotation = true, tag = "ped"}
      H.set(id, 30)
      local p = {id = id, i = i, j = j, k = n, dir = 1, speed = WALK * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.4), state = "walk", stuck = 0}
      if math.random() < 0.5 then p.dir, p.k = -1, k end
      set_target(p, corner(i, j, p.k))
      Ped.peds[id] = p
      return id
    end
    ::continue::
  end
end

-- Which way the road is from where ped `id` stands (a unit x, z), and
-- whether it's mid-block: on the pavement well away from any corner.
function Ped.curb(id)
  local p = Ped.peds[id]
  local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
  if city.network() then
    local e = edges()[p.e]
    local best, cx, cz = math.huge, x, z
    for i = 2, #e.pts do
      local ax, az, bx, bz = e.pts[i - 1][1], e.pts[i - 1][2], e.pts[i][1], e.pts[i][2]
      local dx, dz = bx - ax, bz - az
      local t = math.max(0, math.min(1, ((x - ax) * dx + (z - az) * dz) / (dx * dx + dz * dz)))
      local d = U.dist2d(x, z, ax + dx * t, az + dz * t)
      if d < best then best, cx, cz = d, ax + dx * t, az + dz * t end
    end
    local l = math.max(1e-6, U.dist2d(x, z, cx, cz))
    local jx, jz = city.pos(city.nearest(x, z))
    return (cx - x) / l, (cz - z) / l, U.dist2d(x, z, jx, jz) > 20
  end
  local bx, bz = city.block_center(p.i, p.j)
  local ex, ez = math.abs(x - bx) - (G.city.bx / 2 - 2.2), math.abs(z - bz) - (G.city.bz / 2 - 2.2)
  local mid = (math.abs(ex) < 1 and ez < -7) or (math.abs(ez) < 1 and ex < -7)
  if math.abs(x - bx) / G.city.bx > math.abs(z - bz) / G.city.bz then return x > bx and 1 or -1, 0, mid end
  return 0, z > bz and 1 or -1, mid
end

function Ped.count()
  local n = 0
  for _ in pairs(Ped.peds) do n = n + 1 end
  return n
end

function Ped.start()
  local px, _, pz = U.player_pos()
  for _ = 1, COUNT do spawn_ped(px, pz, 0, 140) end
end

-- ---- behaviour ----------------------------------------------------------------

-- Make every ped within `radius` of (x, z) run away from it.
function Ped.scare(x, z, radius)
  -- Earlier contact/kill handlers may already have despawned the source.
  if not x or not z then return end
  for id, p in pairs(Ped.peds) do
    local px, _, pz = engine.pos(id)
    if U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) < radius then
      if p.state ~= "flee" then
        engine.play("scream", {pos = {px, 1.5, pz}, pitch = 0.8 + math.random() * 0.5})
      end
      p.state, p.until_t = "flee", engine.time() + 3 + math.random() * 2
      p.fx, p.fz = px - x, pz - z
      local l = math.sqrt(p.fx * p.fx + p.fz * p.fz) + 1e-3
      p.fx, p.fz = p.fx / l, p.fz / l
    end
  end
end

local function walk(id, p, dt)
  local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
  local vx, vy, vz = engine.vel(id)
  -- Read the previous physics result before replacing velocity with AI intent.
  -- Sampling after set_vel always saw commanded walking speed, even at a wall.
  local actual_speed2 = vx * vx + vz * vz
  local mx, mz, speed
  if p.state == "flee" then
    if engine.time() > p.until_t then
      p.state = "walk"
      if city.network() then net.rejoin(p, x, z) return end
      p.i, p.j = block_of(x, z)
      p.k = nearest_corner(p, x, z)
      set_target(p, corner(p.i, p.j, p.k))
      return
    end
    mx, mz, speed = p.fx, p.fz, RUN
  else
    if U.dist2d(x, z, p.tx, p.tz) < 0.8 then
      if city.network() then net.next_leg(p) else next_leg(p) end
    end
    mx, mz = p.tx - x, p.tz - z
    local l = math.sqrt(mx * mx + mz * mz) + 1e-3
    mx, mz, speed = mx / l, mz / l, p.speed
    -- Wait at the curb while a car is coming.
    local hit = p.crossing and engine.raycast(x, 0.9, z, mx, 0, mz, 7, id)
    if hit and (engine.tag(hit) == "car" or engine.tag(hit) == "cop") then speed = 0 end
  end
  engine.set_vel(id, mx * speed, vy, mz * speed)
  if speed > 0 then
    local yaw = engine.yaw(id)
    engine.set_yaw(id, yaw + U.angle_diff(yaw, math.atan(mx, mz)) * math.min(1, dt * 10))
  end
  -- Blocked (a lamp, a parked car, a crowd): turn around.
  p.stuck = (speed > 0 and actual_speed2 < 0.1) and p.stuck + dt or 0
  if p.stuck > 2 and p.state == "walk" and city.network() then
    p.stuck = 0
    net.turn(p)
  elseif p.stuck > 2 and p.state == "walk" then
    p.stuck, p.dir = 0, -p.dir
    p.k = (p.k - 1 + p.dir) % 4 + 1
    set_target(p, corner(p.i, p.j, p.k))
  end
end

-- Knocked down: swap the upright walker for a free-tumbling body.
local function knock(id, by, speed)
  local p = Ped.peds[id]
  if not p then return end
  Ped.peds[id] = nil
  local x, y, z = engine.pos(id)
  local yaw = engine.yaw(id)
  local mesh = "ped" .. math.random(VARIANTS)
  engine.despawn(id)
  local vx, vy, vz = engine.vel(by)
  local body = engine.spawn{mesh = mesh, pos = {x, y + 0.2, z}, yaw = yaw, size = {0.7, 1.8, 0.7},
                            body = "dynamic", mass = 70, friction = 0.8, tag = "ped_down"}
  engine.set_vel(body, vx * 0.9, math.max(vy, 0) + 2 + speed * 0.25, vz * 0.9)
  engine.set_angvel(body, (math.random() - 0.5) * 8, (math.random() - 0.5) * 4, (math.random() - 0.5) * 8)
  Ped.down[body] = engine.time() + 12
  engine.emit("ped_down", body, by)
end

engine.on("contact", function(a, b, speed)
  local ped, other
  if Ped.peds[a] then ped, other = a, b elseif Ped.peds[b] then ped, other = b, a else return end
  local tag = engine.tag(other)
  if (tag == "car" or tag == "cop") and speed > 4 then knock(ped, other, speed) end
end)

-- Shot, punched or blown up.
engine.on("killed", function(id, by, dx, dy, dz)
  if not Ped.peds[id] then return end
  Ped.peds[id] = nil
  local body = H.ragdoll(id, "ped" .. math.random(VARIANTS), dx, dy, dz, 5)
  Ped.down[body] = engine.time() + 12
  engine.emit("ped_down", body, by)
end)

engine.on("ped_down", function(_, by)
  if by and (by == G.vehicle or by == G.player) then require("lib.wanted").heat_up(1) end
  local x, _, z = engine.pos(by)
  Ped.scare(x, z, 20)
end)

-- Things that frighten people.
engine.on("key", function(k, down)
  if k == "h" and down and G.vehicle then
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(G.vehicle)
    Ped.scare(x, z, 18)
  end
end)
engine.on("contact", function(a, b, speed)
  if speed > 8 and (engine.tag(a) == "car" or engine.tag(b) == "car") then
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(a)
    Ped.scare(x, z, 15)
  end
end)

local recycle_t = 0
engine.on("tick", function(dt)
  if not G.city then return end
  local px, _, pz = U.player_pos()
  if not px then return end

  -- A fast car heading at someone sends them running.
  local car = G.vehicle
  local cvx, cvz, cx, cz = 0, 0, 0, 0
  if car then
    cx, _, cz = engine.pos(car)
    cvx, _, cvz = engine.vel(car)
  end
  local car_fast = car and cvx * cvx + cvz * cvz > 36

  for id, p in pairs(Ped.peds) do
    if not engine.exists(id) then
      Ped.peds[id] = nil
    else
      if car_fast and p.state ~= "flee" then
        local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
        local dx, dz = x - cx, z - cz
        local d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz
        -- Detect oncoming vehicle up to 24 meters away in the forward velocity cone
        if d2 < 576 and dx * cvx + dz * cvz > 0 then Ped.scare(cx, cz, 4 + math.sqrt(d2)) end
      end
      walk(id, p, dt)
    end
  end

  -- The fallen get up... as someone new, elsewhere.
  local now = engine.time()
  for id, t in pairs(Ped.down) do
    if now > t then
      engine.despawn(id)
      Ped.down[id] = nil
    end
  end

  -- Keep the crowd near the player: recycle the far ones, top up the count.
  recycle_t = recycle_t - dt
  if recycle_t <= 0 then
    recycle_t = 0.5
    for id in pairs(Ped.peds) do
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
      if U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) > 160 then
        engine.despawn(id)
        Ped.peds[id] = nil
        break
      end
    end
    if Ped.count() < COUNT then spawn_ped(px, pz, 70, 140) end
  end
end)

Ped.net = net
return Ped

```

--- FILE: game/systems/police.lua ---
```lua
-- Police: cop cars that hunt the player while the wanted level is up.
-- They path along the street grid toward the player, switch to direct
-- pursuit once they can see you, and bust you if you stop next to one.
-- From two stars up, officers get out and shoot when they catch you.
-- Escape by leaving the search circle unseen. Pure Lua, no engine help.
local G = require "lib.state"
local U = require "lib.util"
local city = require "lib.city"
local vehicle = require "lib.vehicle"
local wanted = require "lib.wanted"
local H = require "lib.health"
local FX = require "lib.fx"

local P = {cops = {}, officers = {}}
local DEPLOY_STARS, DEPLOY_RANGE, SHOOT_RANGE = 2, 28, 35
local COPS_PER_LEVEL = {1, 2, 3, 5, 7}
local SIGHT = 90
local STATION_RANGE = 250 -- a station this close sends some of the backup (it arrives in seconds)
local LANE = 3.2 -- meters right of the center line, as traffic drives

function P.build()
  local parts = require("lib.prefabs").car_parts({0.95, 0.95, 0.97})
  parts[#parts + 1] = {mesh = "cube", pos = {0, -0.2, 0.2}, size = {2.03, 0.3, 2.6}, color = {0.08, 0.08, 0.1}}
  parts[#parts + 1] = {mesh = "cube", pos = {0, 0.71, -0.3}, size = {1.2, 0.06, 0.35}, color = {0.2, 0.2, 0.2}}
  engine.mesh("cop", parts)
  engine.mesh("beacon", {{mesh = "cube", size = {0.5, 0.16, 0.3}, color = {1, 1, 1}, emissive = 1}})
  require("lib.prefabs").person("officer", {0.12, 0.16, 0.36}, {0.07, 0.08, 0.16}, {0.8, 0.62, 0.48}, {0.05, 0.05, 0.08})
  -- A map with a police station has squad cars parked outside it.
  if city.has("police_station") then
    local sx, sz = city.place("police_station")
    for k = -1, 1, 2 do
      engine.spawn{mesh = "cop", pos = {sx + k * 3, 0.75 + U.ground(sx + k * 3, sz), sz}, yaw = math.pi / 2, size = {2, 1.5, 4.5}, body = "static", tag = "prop"}
    end
  end
end

function P.count()
  local n = 0
  for _ in pairs(P.cops) do n = n + 1 end
  return n
end

function P.is_cop(id) return P.cops[id] ~= nil end

-- ---- spawning ------------------------------------------------------------

-- Is something a cop car would land on standing within 6 m of (x, z)?
-- Only what moves counts: scenery (road meshes, lamps, trees) is
-- already off the asphalt.
local function occupied(x, z)
  for _, tag in ipairs{"car", "cop", "player", "officer", "ped"} do
    if engine.nearest(x, 1, z, 6, tag) then return true end
  end
  return false
end

-- How far it is by road from junction `from` to each junction (meters).
local function road_dist(from)
  local dist, done = {[from] = 0}, {}
  while true do
    local best, u = math.huge
    for j, d in pairs(dist) do
      if not done[j] and (d < best or (d == best and j < u)) then best, u = d, j end
    end
    if not u then return dist end
    done[u] = true
    for _, n in ipairs(city.links(u)) do
      local p, len = city.path(u, n), 0
      for i = 2, #p do len = len + U.dist2d(p[i - 1][1], p[i - 1][2], p[i][1], p[i][2]) end
      if not dist[n] or best + len < dist[n] then dist[n] = best + len end
    end
  end
end

-- A junction 70-130 m from where the cops think the player is (further,
-- in a town whose junctions are far apart). On a map drawn as roads,
-- 70-160 m by road (up to 320 up a long dead end): a junction that's close as the crow flies can be a long
-- drive round, and a cop sent the long way ends up too far off and is
-- recycled, again and again.
local function spawn_point()
  local px, pz, py = wanted.center[1], wanted.center[2], wanted.center[3]
  -- Not down in a tunnel when they're up in the street, or the other way.
  local function level(j)
    local y = city.level(j)
    return not (py and y) or math.abs(y - py) < 4
  end
  if city.network() then
    -- Up a long dead end there may be none that near: then the nearest
    -- by road, up to twice as far.
    local dist, cands = road_dist(city.nearest(px, pz, py)), {}
    for _, far in ipairs{160, 320} do
      for _, j in ipairs(city.junctions()) do
        local x, z = city.pos(j)
        local d = dist[j]
        if d and d >= 70 and d <= far and U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) >= 50 and not occupied(x, z) and level(j) then
          cands[#cands + 1] = j
        end
      end
      if #cands > 0 then break end
    end
    if #cands > 0 then
      local j = cands[math.random(#cands)]
      local x, z = city.pos(j)
      -- Align police squad car along outgoing road lane rather than direct line-of-sight pointing
      local links = city.links(j)
      local yaw = math.atan(px - x, pz - z)
      if links and #links > 0 then
        local best_dot, best_yaw = -2, yaw
        for _, nxt in ipairs(links) do
          local path = city.path(j, nxt)
          if path and #path >= 2 then
            local lx, lz = path[2][1] - x, path[2][2] - z
            local lyaw = math.atan(lx, lz)
            local fx, fz = U.forward(lyaw)
            local dx, dz = px - x, pz - z
            local dl = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz) + 1e-5
            local dot = (fx * dx + fz * dz) / dl
            if dot > best_dot then best_dot, best_yaw = dot, lyaw end
          end
        end
        yaw = best_yaw
      end
      -- From further off, time to get here before it may be recycled.
      return x, z, yaw, city.level(j), U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) > 150 and 25 or nil
    end
  end
  for _ = 1, 10 do
    local j = city.random_near(px, pz, 70, 130) or city.random_near(px, pz, 40, 260)
    if not j then return end
    local x, z = city.pos(j)
    if not occupied(x, z) and level(j) then return x, z, math.atan(px - x, pz - z), city.level(j) end
  end
  -- A town's junctions can be few and far apart (and busy): anywhere
  -- along a road in range will do, in the lane heading for the player.
  if not city.network() then return end
  local roads = city.roads()
  for _ = 1, 20 do
    local r = roads[math.random(#roads)]
    local path = city.path(r[1], r[2])
    local i = math.random(2, #path)
    local a, b = path[i - 1], path[i]
    local t = math.random()
    local x, z = a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t
    local d = U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz)
    local y = city.height_on(r[1], r[2], x, z)
    local yaw = math.atan(b[1] - a[1], b[2] - a[2])
    local rx, rz = U.right(yaw)
    x, z = x + rx * 3.2, z + rz * 3.2
    if d >= 60 and d <= 200 and not occupied(x, z) and not (py and y and math.abs(y - py) >= 4) then
      return x, z, yaw, y
    end
  end
end


-- Out of the station's yard onto the nearest road, if the station is close
-- enough to send a unit from.
local function station_exit(px, pz)
  if not (city.network() and city.has("police_station")) then return end
  local sx, sz = city.place("police_station")
  if U.dist2d(sx, sz, px, pz) > STATION_RANGE then return end
  local roadnet = require "lib.roadnet"
  local _, _, rx, rz = roadnet.nearest_edge(sx, sz)
  if occupied(rx, rz) then return end
  return rx, rz, math.atan(px - rx, pz - rz)
end

local function spawn_cop()
  local px, _, pz = U.player_pos()
  local x, z, yaw, launch, y
  local stars = wanted.stars()
  local is_military_pasi = (stars >= 5 and math.random() < 0.6)

  -- 5-star military response: deploy from Pansio naval garrison gate if present on map
  if is_military_pasi and city.has and city.has("heist_start") then
    x, z, yaw = -348.4, 2583.1, 1.85
    launch = 45
  elseif px and P.count() >= 1 then
    local sx, sz, syaw = station_exit(px, pz)
    if sx and math.random() < 0.5 then -- rolled for only with a station in reach
      x, z, yaw = sx, sz, syaw
      launch = 30 -- seconds to get to the player before it may be recycled
    end
  end
  if not x then x, z, yaw, y, launch = spawn_point() end
  if not x then return end
  local mesh = (is_military_pasi and city.has and city.has("heist_start")) and "pasi" or "cop"
  local id = vehicle.spawn(x, z, yaw, nil, mesh, "cop", y)
  vehicle.headlights(id)
  local c = {id = id, stuck = 0, reverse = 0, launch = launch, is_pasi = (mesh == "pasi")}
  c.red = engine.spawn{mesh = "beacon", color = {1, 0.1, 0.1}}
  c.blue = engine.spawn{mesh = "beacon", color = {0.1, 0.2, 1}}
  c.siren = engine.play("siren", {loop = true, follow = id})
  P.cops[id] = c
end

local function remove(id)
  local c = P.cops[id]
  if not c then return end
  engine.despawn(c.red)
  engine.despawn(c.blue)
  if c.siren then engine.stop(c.siren) end
  P.cops[id] = nil
end

function P.clear()
  for id in pairs(P.cops) do
    remove(id)
    engine.despawn(id)
  end
  for id in pairs(P.officers) do engine.despawn(id) end
  P.officers = {}
end

function P.officer_count()
  local n = 0
  for _ in pairs(P.officers) do n = n + 1 end
  return n
end

-- ---- perception ----------------------------------------------------------

local function sees_player(id)
  local target = G.vehicle or G.player
  if not target then return false end
  local x, y, z = engine.pos(id)
  local px, py, pz = engine.pos(target)
  local dx, dy, dz = px - x, py + 0.4 - (y + 1), pz - z
  local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
  if d > SIGHT then return false end
  local hit = engine.raycast(x, y + 1, z, dx, dy, dz, d, id)
  return hit == nil or hit == target
end

-- ---- driving -------------------------------------------------------------

-- Sight and driving must agree: ignoring a visible off-road target just
-- because it is 50-90m away sends cars searching away from it.
function P.direct_pursuit(sees, dist) return sees and dist < SIGHT end

-- On a map drawn as roads, a car out between junctions drives its own
-- road to whichever end is fewer roads from the goal, rather than heading
-- straight for the nearest junction (which may be across back gardens).
local function road_route(x, y, z, goal)
  local roadnet = require "lib.roadnet"
  local hops, queue = {[goal] = 0}, {goal}
  for _, q in ipairs(queue) do
    for _, nb in ipairs(city.links(q)) do
      if not hops[nb] then hops[nb] = hops[q] + 1; queue[#queue + 1] = nb end
    end
  end
  local e, i = roadnet.nearest_edge(x, z, nil, y)
  local E = roadnet.net().edges[e]
  local route = {}
  if (hops[E.b] or math.huge) <= (hops[E.a] or math.huge) then
    for k = i, #E.pts do route[#route + 1] = E.pts[k] end
  else
    for k = i - 1, 1, -1 do route[#route + 1] = E.pts[k] end
  end
  return route
end

local function drive_cop(c, dt)
  local id = c.id
  local x, y, z = engine.pos(id)
  local px, py, pz = U.player_pos()
  local yaw = engine.yaw(id)
  local speed = vehicle.speed(id)
  local dist = U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz)
  local tx, tz, want

  if P.direct_pursuit(c.sees, dist) then
    -- Direct pursuit, leading the target a little.
    local vx, _, vz = engine.vel(G.vehicle or G.player)
    tx, tz = px + vx * 0.4, pz + vz * 0.4
    want = dist < 12 and math.max(0, dist - 4) or 30
  else
    -- Follow the roads one junction at a time toward the player if seen,
    -- otherwise toward the last sighting; once there, search nearby streets.
    -- A route may start out away from them, round water or a park.
    -- (Nearest at the same level: not the tunnel under a street, or the
    -- street over it.)
    local at = city.nearest(x, z, y)
    local goal = city.nearest(wanted.seen and px or wanted.center[1], wanted.seen and pz or wanted.center[2],
                              wanted.seen and py or wanted.center[3])
    if c.goal and U.dist2d(x, z, c.goal[1], c.goal[2]) < 9 and c.ri < #c.route then
      c.ri = c.ri + 1 -- round the next bend of this road
      c.goal = c.route[c.ri]
    elseif city.network() and (not c.goal or U.dist2d(x, z, c.goal[1], c.goal[2]) < 9)
        and U.dist2d(x, z, city.pos(at)) > 15 then
      c.route, c.ri = road_route(x, y, z, goal), 1
      c.goal = c.route[1]
    elseif not c.goal or U.dist2d(x, z, c.goal[1], c.goal[2]) < 9 then
      local from = at
      if at == goal then
        local opts = city.links(at) -- there: search a street nearby
        at = opts[math.random(math.max(1, #opts))] or at
      else
        at = city.step_toward(at, goal)
      end
      -- Pick the road up at the bend after the stretch the car is on.
      c.route, c.ri = city.path(from, at), 2
      local best = math.huge
      for i = 2, #c.route do
        local d = U.dist2d(x, z, c.route[i][1], c.route[i][2]) + U.dist2d(x, z, c.route[i - 1][1], c.route[i - 1][2])
        if d < best then best, c.ri = d, i end
      end
      c.goal = c.route[c.ri]
    end
    -- Drive in the right-hand lane, like traffic, so oncoming cars pass
    -- by; pull out into the other lane to overtake whatever's ahead in ours.
    local gx, gz = c.goal[1], c.goal[2]
    local rx, rz = U.right(math.atan(gx - x, gz - z))
    local fx, fz = U.forward(yaw)
    local hit, d = engine.raycast(x + fx * 2.5, 0.8, z + fz * 2.5, fx, 0, fz, 16, id)
    local tag = hit and engine.tag(hit)
    if (tag == "car" or tag == "cop") and math.cos(U.angle_diff(yaw, engine.yaw(hit))) > 0.5 then c.overtake = 2 end
    -- On a road map, a parked or stuck car in the lane is passed too.
```
