```lua
    if (tag == "car" or tag == "cop") and city.network() and math.abs(vehicle.speed(hit)) < 1 then c.overtake = 2 end
    c.overtake = math.max(0, (c.overtake or 0) - dt)
    local lane = c.overtake > 0 and -LANE or LANE
    tx, tz = gx + rx * lane, gz + rz * lane
    -- Slow for the corner coming up at the next intersection, and for
    -- anything in the way.
    want = U.dist2d(x, z, gx, gz) < 22 and 13 or 30
    if hit and tag ~= "player" and d < 8 then want = math.min(want, 8) end
  end

  local diff = U.angle_diff(yaw, math.atan(tx - x, tz - z))
  local throttle = speed < want and 1 or (speed > want + 3 and -1 or 0)
  if math.abs(diff) > 1.1 and speed > 12 then throttle = -1 end
  local input = {throttle = throttle, steer = U.clamp(diff * 2.5, -1, 1), handbrake = math.abs(diff) > 0.9 and speed > 14}

  -- Wedged against something: back up with opposite lock for a second.
  c.stuck = (throttle > 0 and math.abs(speed) < 1.5) and c.stuck + dt or 0
  if c.stuck > 1.2 then
    c.reverse, c.stuck = 1.0, 0
    -- Wedged again and again on a road map (a U-turn on a narrow street):
    -- back up further, a proper three-point turn, and think again about
    -- the way.
    if city.network() then
      c.jams = (c.jams or 0) + 1
      if c.jams >= 2 then c.reverse = 2.2 end
      -- Swung wide round a corner into a wall: back to the middle of the
      -- junction, where there's room, then on along the road.
      if c.jams == 2 and c.route then
        local jx, jz = city.pos(city.nearest(x, z, y))
        if U.dist2d(x, z, jx, jz) < 25 then
          table.insert(c.route, c.ri, {jx, jz})
          c.goal = c.route[c.ri]
        end
      end
      if c.jams >= 4 then c.goal, c.jams = nil, 0 end
    end
  end
  if c.reverse > 0 then
    c.reverse = c.reverse - dt
    input = {throttle = -1, steer = -input.steer}
  end
  vehicle.drive(id, dt, input)
end

-- ---- officers on foot ----------------------------------------------------------
-- From two stars up, cops that catch up with you get out and shoot.

local function deploy(c)
  c.deployed = true
  local x, y, z = engine.pos(c.id)
  local rx, _, rz = engine.right(c.id)
  for _, side in ipairs{-1, 1} do
    local id = engine.spawn{mesh = "officer", pos = {x + rx * side * 2.2, y + 0.4, z + rz * side * 2.2}, size = {0.7, 1.8, 0.7},
                            body = "dynamic", mass = 85, friction = 0, step = 0.45, lock_rotation = true, tag = "officer"}
    H.set(id, 60)
    P.officers[id] = {car = c.id, cool = 0.6 + math.random()}
  end
end

local function recall(c)
  c.deployed = false
  for id, o in pairs(P.officers) do
    if o.car == c.id then
      engine.despawn(id)
      P.officers[id] = nil
    end
  end
end

local function officer_shoot(id, o)
  local target = G.vehicle or G.player
  local x, y, z = engine.pos(id)
  local tx, ty, tz = engine.pos(target)
  local d = U.dist2d(x, z, tx, tz)
  local vx, _, vz = engine.vel(target)
  local chance = U.clamp(0.65 - d / 55 - math.sqrt(vx * vx + vz * vz) * 0.02, 0.08, 0.65)
  local hit = math.random() < chance
  local ex, ey, ez = tx, ty + 0.3, tz
  if not hit then ex, ey, ez = tx + (math.random() - 0.5) * 3, ty + math.random() * 1.5, tz + (math.random() - 0.5) * 3 end
  local mx, my, mz = x, y + 0.5, z
  FX.flash(mx + (tx - x) / d * 0.6, my, mz + (tz - z) / d * 0.6, 0.25)
  FX.tracer(mx, my, mz, ex, ey, ez)
  engine.play("pistol", {pos = {x, y, z}, volume = 0.8})
  if hit then
    if G.vehicle then H.damage(G.vehicle, 5, id) else H.hurt_player(7, id) end
  end
end

local function run_officers(dt, px, pz)
  local seen, close = false, false
  for id, o in pairs(P.officers) do
    if not engine.exists(id) then
      P.officers[id] = nil
    else
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
      local d = U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz)
      local sees = sees_player(id)
      seen = seen or sees
      local _, vy = engine.vel(id)
      local dx, dz = px - x, pz - z
      engine.set_yaw(id, math.atan(dx, dz))
      if sees and d < SHOOT_RANGE and d > 6 then
        -- Stand and shoot.
        engine.set_vel(id, 0, vy, 0)
        o.cool = o.cool - dt
        if o.cool <= 0 then
          o.cool = 0.9 + math.random() * 0.5
          officer_shoot(id, o)
        end
      else
        -- Close in (or come around the corner).
        local s = d > 2 and 5 or 0
        engine.set_vel(id, dx / (d + 1e-3) * s, vy, dz / (d + 1e-3) * s)
      end
      if d < 2.5 then close = true end
    end
  end
  return seen, close
end

engine.on("killed", function(id, by, dx, dy, dz)
  if not P.officers[id] then return end
  P.officers[id] = nil
  local body = H.ragdoll(id, "officer", dx, dy, dz, 5)
  require("systems.peds").down[body] = engine.time() + 12
  if by == G.player or by == G.vehicle then wanted.add(1) end
end)

-- Run them over and they go down too.
engine.on("contact", function(a, b, speed)
  local o, other
  if P.officers[a] then o, other = a, b elseif P.officers[b] then o, other = b, a else return end
  local tag = engine.tag(other)
  if (tag == "car" or tag == "cop") and speed > 4 then
    local vx, vy, vz = engine.vel(other)
    H.damage(o, 200, other, vx, vy, vz)
  end
end)

-- ---- the loop ------------------------------------------------------------

local spawn_timer, busted_for = 0, 0

local function busted()
  local fine = math.min(G.cash, 100 + math.floor(G.cash * 0.1))
  G.cash = G.cash - fine
  engine.emit("busted", fine)
  wanted.clear()
  P.clear()
  -- Released on foot outside the station: the map's police station, or the
  -- start of the game.
  if G.vehicle then
    engine.set_vel(G.vehicle, 0, 0, 0)
    G.vehicle = nil
  elseif G.player then
    engine.despawn(G.player)
  end
  if city.has("police_station") then
    local sx, sz = city.place("police_station")
    require("lib.player").spawn(sx, 1.2 + U.ground(sx, sz + 5), sz + 5, 0)
  else
    require("lib.player").spawn(G.spawn[1], G.spawn[2], G.spawn[3], 0)
  end
  U.message("BUSTED", 4)
  U.message(string.format("-$%d", fine), 4)
  busted_for = 0
end

engine.on("tick", function(dt)
  -- Cops that are gone, wrecked, or far away get recycled.
  local px, py, pz = U.player_pos()
  if not px then return end
  for id, c in pairs(P.cops) do
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
    local wrecked = not engine.exists(id) or vehicle.upside_down(id)
    c.wrecked_for = wrecked and (c.wrecked_for or 0) + dt or 0
    c.launch = c.launch and c.launch > 0 and c.launch - dt or nil
    if not x or c.wrecked_for > 3 or (U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) > 170 and not c.launch) then
      remove(id)
      if x then engine.despawn(id) end
    end
  end

  if wanted.stars() == 0 then
    -- Off duty: idle, and leave once out of sight.
    for id, o in pairs(P.officers) do
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
      if not x or U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) > 50 then
        if x then engine.despawn(id) end
        P.officers[id] = nil
      end
    end
    for id, c in pairs(P.cops) do
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
      vehicle.drive(id, dt, {throttle = 0})
      if U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz) > 60 then
        remove(id)
        engine.despawn(id)
      end
    end
    return
  end

  -- Keep the right number of units on the street.
  spawn_timer = spawn_timer - dt
  if spawn_timer <= 0 and P.count() < COPS_PER_LEVEL[wanted.stars()] then
    spawn_timer = 1.5
    spawn_cop()
  end

  local seen, close = run_officers(dt, px, pz)
  -- An officer may just have killed the player; nothing more to chase this tick.
  if not (G.player or G.vehicle) then return end
  local slow_player = G.player ~= nil or math.abs(vehicle.speed(G.vehicle)) < 4
  for id, c in pairs(P.cops) do
    c.siren = c.siren or engine.play("siren", {loop = true, follow = id})
    c.sees = sees_player(id)
    seen = seen or c.sees
    local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
    local d = U.dist2d(x, z, px, pz)
    if c.deployed then
      vehicle.drive(id, dt, {throttle = 0, handbrake = true})
      local crew = false
      for oid, o in pairs(P.officers) do
        if o.car == id and engine.exists(oid) then crew = true break end
      end
      if d > 60 or wanted.stars() < DEPLOY_STARS then recall(c)
      elseif crew then c.redeploy = nil
      elseif not c.redeploy then c.redeploy = engine.time() + 5 -- its officers are down: a fresh pair soon
      elseif engine.time() >= c.redeploy and c.sees and d < DEPLOY_RANGE then
        c.redeploy = nil
        deploy(c)
      end
    else
      drive_cop(c, dt)
      if wanted.stars() >= DEPLOY_STARS and c.sees and d < DEPLOY_RANGE and slow_player then deploy(c) end
      if d < 7 and math.abs(vehicle.speed(id)) < 6 then close = true end
    end
  end

  -- Evasion: unseen and outside the search circle for a few seconds.
  if seen then
    wanted.seen, wanted.unseen_for, wanted.center = true, 0, {px, pz, py}
  else
    wanted.seen = false
    wanted.unseen_for = wanted.unseen_for + dt
    local outside = U.dist2d(px, pz, wanted.center[1], wanted.center[2]) > wanted.radius()
    if (outside and wanted.unseen_for > 3) or wanted.unseen_for > 30 then
      wanted.clear()
      U.message("LOST THE COPS", 3)
      return
    end
  end

  -- Busted: stopped (or on foot) right next to a cop.
  local slow = G.player ~= nil or math.abs(vehicle.speed(G.vehicle)) < 2
  busted_for = (close and slow) and busted_for + dt or 0
  if busted_for > 1.5 then busted() end
end)

-- Stealing a cop car is its own crime.
engine.on("vehicle_enter", function(id)
  if P.cops[id] then
    remove(id)
    engine.set_visible(id, true)
    wanted.add(1)
  end
end)

engine.on("wanted_changed", function(new)
  if new == 0 then
    for _, c in pairs(P.cops) do engine.stop(c.siren); c.siren = nil end
  end
end)

-- Light bars ride on the roof and alternate red/blue.
engine.on("frame", function()
  local on = math.floor(engine.time() * 6) % 2 == 0
  local active = wanted.stars() > 0
  for id, c in pairs(P.cops) do
    -- A car blown up this tick is only dropped from P.cops on the next one.
    if not engine.exists(id) then goto continue end
    local x, y, z = engine.pos(id)
    local ux, uy, uz = engine.up(id)
    local rx, ry, rz = engine.right(id)
    local fx, fy, fz = engine.forward(id)
    local qx, qy, qz, qw = engine.rot(id)
    for side, light in pairs{[1] = c.red, [-1] = c.blue} do
      local s = side * 0.3
      engine.set_pos(light, x + ux * 0.8 + rx * s - fx * 0.3, y + uy * 0.8 + ry * s - fy * 0.3, z + uz * 0.8 + rz * s - fz * 0.3)
      engine.set_rot(light, qx, qy, qz, qw)
      local lit = active and (on == (side == 1))
      local k = lit and 1 or 0.25
      if side == 1 then engine.set_color(light, k, 0.1 * k, 0.1 * k) else engine.set_color(light, 0.1 * k, 0.2 * k, k) end
    end
    ::continue::
  end
end)

-- Blips and the search circle on the minimap.
engine.on("minimap", function(map)
  local blink = math.floor(engine.time() * 4) % 2 == 0
  for id in pairs(P.cops) do
    if engine.exists(id) then
      local x, _, z = engine.pos(id)
      local mx, my = map(x, z)
      engine.rect(mx - 2.5, my - 2.5, 5, 5, blink and 1 or 0.2, 0.1, blink and 0.2 or 1, 1)
    end
  end
  if wanted.stars() > 0 then
    local r = wanted.radius()
    for k = 0, 47 do
      local a = k / 48 * math.pi * 2
      local mx, my = map(wanted.center[1] + math.cos(a) * r, wanted.center[2] + math.sin(a) * r)
      engine.rect(mx - 1, my - 1, 2, 2, 1, 0.3, 0.3, wanted.seen and 0.9 or 0.5)
    end
  end
end)

return P

```

--- FILE: game/systems/aurajoki.lua ---
```lua
-- Turku's Aurajoki: the restaurant boats on its quays, Suomen Joutsen at
-- Forum Marinum, the Föri ferry, a speedboat to take, and the cathedral's
-- bells at noon.
local U = require "lib.util"
local vehicle = require "lib.vehicle"

local A = {
  bridges = {},
  riverboats = {},
  fori = nil,
}

-- The Föri's quays on either bank.
local FORI_ROUTE = {
  bank_east = {-1166.2, 1174.0},  -- Tervatori (Itäinen Rantakatu)
  bank_west = {-1258.2, 1117.2},  -- Wechterinkuja (Läntinen Rantakatu)
  yaw = -2.13,
}

function A.build(map)
  if not map or (map.id ~= "turku" and map.name ~= "Turku") then return end

  -- The river, its quays and its bridges are the map's own, from
  -- OpenStreetMap and the LiDAR terrain (lib/roadnet); this adds what floats
  -- on it. Every spot here is on the surveyed water (test/aurajoki_probe).
  local R = require "lib.roadnet"
  for _, e in ipairs(R.net().edges) do
    if e.deck and e.height and e.street then
      local a, b = e.pts[1], e.pts[#e.pts]
      local mx, mz = (a[1] + b[1]) / 2, (a[2] + b[2]) / 2
      local w = R.water_level(mx, mz)
      if w then A.bridges[#A.bridges + 1] = {name = e.street, pos = {mx, mz}, deck = e.height(mx, mz), water = w} end
    end
  end

  -- Restaurant boats along the quay between Auransilta and Myllysilta, as they lie in summer.
  local boat_moorings = {
    {pos = {-101, 356}, yaw = -1.0, name = "Donna"},
    {pos = {-177, 396}, yaw = -1.0, name = "Papa Joe"},
    {pos = {-254, 436}, yaw = -1.2, name = "Aussie Bar"},
    {pos = {-322, 472}, yaw = -1.3, name = "Cindy"},
    {pos = {-410, 521}, yaw = -1.3, name = "Svarte Rudolf"},
  }
  for _, m in ipairs(boat_moorings) do
    local bid = engine.spawn{
      mesh = "riverboat",
      pos = {m.pos[1], 0.1, m.pos[2]},
      yaw = m.yaw,
      tag = "prop",
      body = "static",
    }
    A.riverboats[#A.riverboats + 1] = bid
  end

  -- Suomen Joutsen moored at Forum Marinum
  engine.spawn{
    mesh = "suomen_joutsen",
    pos = {-1708, 0.2, 1601},
    yaw = -0.7,
    tag = "ship",
    body = "static",
  }

  -- It runs between the quays, on the part of the line that's water, its
  -- ramps a few meters short of either side.
  local p0, p1 = FORI_ROUTE.bank_east, FORI_ROUTE.bank_west
  local t0, t1
  for k = 0, 100 do
    local t = k / 100
    if R.water_level(p0[1] + (p1[1] - p0[1]) * t, p0[2] + (p1[2] - p0[2]) * t) then t0, t1 = t0 or t, t end
  end
  local pad = 5.5 / U.dist2d(p0[1], p0[2], p1[1], p1[2])
  t0, t1 = (t0 or 0) + pad, (t1 or 1) - pad
  local init_progress = 0.45
  local fx = p0[1] + (p1[1] - p0[1]) * (t0 + (t1 - t0) * init_progress)
  local fz = p0[2] + (p1[2] - p0[2]) * (t0 + (t1 - t0) * init_progress)
  local fori_id = engine.spawn{
    mesh = "fori",
    pos = {fx, 0.45, fz},
    yaw = FORI_ROUTE.yaw,
    size = {10.5, 1.2, 9.2},
    tag = "ferry",
    body = "kinematic",
  }
  A.fori = {
    id = fori_id,
    target = 1, -- 0 for bank_east, 1 for bank_west
    progress = init_progress,
    t0 = t0, t1 = t1,
    pause = 0.0,
  }

  -- A Puskuri speedboat to take, by Auransilta
  local boat_id = vehicle.spawn(-45, 320, -1.8, nil, "speedboat", "boat")
  A.speedboat = boat_id

  -- 6. Aurajoki Riverbank Promenade: Stone Quays, Willows, Bollards & Lamps
  -- Follows both banks from Satama/Forum Marinum past Tuomiokirkko to Koroistenniemi.
  local river_banks = {
    -- West bank (Läntinen Rantakatu & river path)
    west = {
      {-2150, 1720}, {-1850, 1520}, {-1550, 1330}, {-1258, 1117},
      {-980, 920}, {-820, 770}, {-680, 670}, {-460, 520},
      {-310, 430}, {-150, 350}, {-25, 295}, {120, 150},
      {250, -40}, {360, -320}, {420, -680}, {460, -950},
      {620, -1250}, {880, -1550}, {1120, -1750}, {1210, -1860}
    },
    -- East bank (Itäinen Rantakatu & river path)
    east = {
      {-2050, 1820}, {-1750, 1610}, {-1450, 1410}, {-1166, 1174},
      {-920, 970}, {-760, 820}, {-620, 720}, {-400, 580},
      {-250, 480}, {-100, 400}, {25, 345}, {180, 200},
      {300, 10}, {420, -260}, {490, -640}, {540, -920},
      {720, -1210}, {980, -1510}, {1200, -1710}, {1280, -1820}
    }
  }

  local quay_stone = {0.52, 0.50, 0.48}
  local bollard_iron = {0.18, 0.18, 0.20}

  for bank_name, pts in pairs(river_banks) do
    for i = 2, #pts do
      local p0, p1 = pts[i - 1], pts[i]
      local len = U.dist2d(p0[1], p0[2], p1[1], p1[2])
      local yaw = math.atan(p1[1] - p0[1], p1[2] - p0[2])
      local n_steps = math.max(1, math.floor(len / 16.0))

      for s = 0, n_steps - 1 do
        local t = s / n_steps
        local x = p0[1] + (p1[1] - p0[1]) * t
        local z = p0[2] + (p1[2] - p0[2]) * t
        local y = U.ground(x, z)

        -- Urban stone quay section (Forum Marinum to Tuomiokirkko)
        if z > -400 and z < 1700 then
          -- Low granite edge quay barrier
          engine.spawn{
            mesh = "cube",
            pos = {x, y + 0.35, z},
            yaw = yaw,
            size = {1.2, 0.7, 15.8},
            color = quay_stone,
            tag = "wall",
            body = "static",
          }

          -- Quayside cast-iron mooring bollard every other segment
          if s % 2 == 0 then
            engine.spawn{
              mesh = "cylinder",
              pos = {x, y + 0.8, z},
              size = {0.35, 0.6, 0.35},
              color = bollard_iron,
              tag = "prop",
              body = "static",
            }
          end

          -- Riverside promenade weeping willows & linden trees
          local rx, rz = U.right(yaw)
          local tx = x + rx * (bank_name == "west" and -4.5 or 4.5)
          local tz = z + rz * (bank_name == "west" and -4.5 or 4.5)
          local ty = U.ground(tx, tz)
          if not R.wet(tx, tz) then
            engine.spawn{
              mesh = (s % 2 == 0) and "willow_tree" or "tree",
              pos = {tx, ty, tz},
              scale = 0.95 + (s % 3) * 0.15,
              tag = "tree",
            }
          end

          -- Promenade park benches & night lighting
          if s % 3 == 0 then
            local bx = x + rx * (bank_name == "west" and -3.2 or 3.2)
            local bz = z + rz * (bank_name == "west" and -3.2 or 3.2)
            engine.spawn{
              mesh = "park_bench",
              pos = {bx, U.ground(bx, bz), bz},
              yaw = yaw + (bank_name == "west" and math.pi/2 or -math.pi/2),
              tag = "prop",
            }
            engine.spawn{
              mesh = "lamp",
              pos = {bx, U.ground(bx, bz), bz},
              yaw = yaw,
              tag = "lamp",
            }
          end
        else
          -- Natural green riverbank upstream toward Koroistenniemi
          local rx, rz = U.right(yaw)
          local tx = x + rx * (bank_name == "west" and -6.0 or 6.0)
          local tz = z + rz * (bank_name == "west" and -6.0 or 6.0)
          local ty = U.ground(tx, tz)
          if not R.wet(tx, tz) then
            engine.spawn{
              mesh = "tree",
              pos = {tx, ty, tz},
              scale = 1.1 + (s % 2) * 0.2,
              tag = "tree",
            }
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  -- 7. Koroistenniemi Historic Memorial Cross & Bishop's Knoll
  -- Sits on the elevated promontory where Vähäjoki meets Aurajoki
  local kx, kz = 1222.0, -1865.0
  local ky = U.ground(kx, kz)
  engine.spawn{
    mesh = "koroisten_risti",
    pos = {kx, ky, kz},
    yaw = 0.45,
    tag = "landmark",
    body = "static",
  }
end

-- Update Föri ferry position across Aurajoki
function A.tick(dt)
  if not A.fori then return end
  local f = A.fori
  if f.pause > 0 then
    f.pause = f.pause - dt
    return
  end

  local speed = 0.08 * dt -- crossing takes ~12.5 seconds
  if f.target == 1 then
    f.progress = math.min(1.0, f.progress + speed)
    if f.progress >= 1.0 then
      f.target = 0
      f.pause = 6.0 -- boarding pause on west bank
    end
  else
    f.progress = math.max(0.0, f.progress - speed)
    if f.progress <= 0.0 then
      f.target = 1
      f.pause = 6.0 -- boarding pause on east bank
    end
  end

  local p0, p1 = FORI_ROUTE.bank_east, FORI_ROUTE.bank_west
  local t = f.t0 + (f.t1 - f.t0) * f.progress
  local cur_x = p0[1] + (p1[1] - p0[1]) * t
  local cur_z = p0[2] + (p1[2] - p0[2]) * t
  if engine.exists(f.id) then
    engine.set_pos(f.id, cur_x, 0.25, cur_z)
  end
end

-- The cathedral's bells ring noon over the river.
local CATHEDRAL = {380, -90}
local last_hour
engine.on("tick", function(dt)
  A.tick(dt or (1/60))
  if not A.fori then return end
  local hour = math.floor(require("lib.daynight").hour)
  if last_hour and hour ~= last_hour and hour == 12 then
    engine.play("cathedral_bell", {pos = {CATHEDRAL[1], 25, CATHEDRAL[2]}, volume = 0.8})
  end
  last_hour = hour
end)

return A

```
